### [86] [Weight Certificates for Convex Multi-Objective MPC: Geometric Characterization, $\ell^1$ Construction, and $\ell^2$ Foreclosure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12520)
*Hadi Hajieghrary,Benedikt Walter,Chaitanya Shinde,Miguel Hurtadoand Jerry Lopez*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究自动驾驶规则手册中词典序规则违反排序与模型预测控制（MPC）加权求和实现之间的关系。作者证明传统分离启发式w1≫w2≫⋯≫wL回答的是错误问题，给出了加权求和精确复现词典序最优的充要条件（支持函数/外法锥条件），并针对hinge与squared-hinge惩罚分别推导出权重可行域的几何刻画及近似收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶规则手册通常以词典序（lexicographic）排列规则违反，而MPC控制中常通过加权求和近似实现该排序。传统做法依赖w1≫w2≫⋯≫wL的分离启发式，但这一启发式缺乏理论依据，可能导致权重选择不精确或不稳定，影响自动驾驶安全性与规则遵守度。

Method: 采用凸优化与变分分析框架。首先将词典序优化与加权求和最优解的等价性转化为几何条件：权重（加上单位性能系数）需支撑achievement map在词典序点处的上像（upper image），可行权重构成外法锥的单位性能切片。对hinge惩罚，利用缩放KKT系统投影得到多面体切片，通过线性规划求解带认证裕度的内部权重；对squared-hinge惩罚，推导出当极限乘子非零时不存在有限精确权重，且违反量沿局部极小分支以O(1/w)衰减。

Result: 在11个可校准场景类中，9个场景类的所得权重近似等分各层分量；在25条规则的闭环nuPlan实验中，相较匹配的启发式权重，合法规层事件精度约翻倍。但证书是逐点（pointwise）有效的：单个权重无法在整个episode的所有采样tick上保持有效，中位有效寿命仅为单个采样间隔（原生采样率下后续tick为零），持久性取决于主动集稳定性。合规模式监控器仅能检测到部分实测失效。

Conclusion: 分离启发式w1≫w2≫⋯≫wL在理论上不成立，精确权重需要针对achievement map的外法锥结构进行求解。由于证书的逐点性，建议采用带选择性级联回退（selective cascade fallback）的监控加权求解方案，尽管合规模式监控器覆盖能力有限。

Abstract: Automated-driving rulebooks rank rule violations lexicographically, and model predictive control enforces that ranking either exactly, through $L{+}1$ sequential programs per tick, or approximately, through a weighted sum tuned by the separation heuristic $w_1\gg w_2\gg\cdots\gg w_L$. We show the heuristic answers the wrong question. For a convex priority-ordered program, a weighted sum reproduces the lexicographic optimum precisely when its weight, augmented by a unit performance coefficient, supports the upper image of the achievement map at the lexicographic point; the admissible weights form the unit-performance slice of an outward normal cone. Under hinge penalties this slice is a polyhedron obtained by projecting a scaled-KKT system, and a linear program returns an interior weight with a certified margin; under squared-hinge penalties no finite weight is exact whenever the limiting multiplier is nonzero, with violation along the local minimizer branch decaying as $O(1/w)$. Calibrated on held-out logs, the resulting weights have near-equal tier components in nine of eleven calibration-eligible scenario classes and roughly double legal-tier event precision against a matched heuristic weight in closed-loop nuPlan experiments on a 25-rule rulebook. The certificate is, however, pointwise: no single weight is valid across the sampled ticks of an episode, the median lifetime is one sampling interval (zero subsequent ticks at the native rate), and persistence tracks active-set stability. These findings motivate monitored weighted solves with selective cascade fallback, although the compliance-pattern monitor detects only a subset of measured lapses.

</details>


### [87] [Optimal Heat Storage Sizing for District Heating Networks to Maximize Electricity Revenue from Combined Heat and Power Units](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12525)
*Martin Sollich,Maarten Blommaert*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于数学优化的可扩展自动化方法，用于在区域供热网络(DHN)中优化短期储热容量。该非线性物基方法同时建模DHN、热生产者和储热系统，以最小化总经济成本。在比利时某第三代热电联产DHN（30个用户）上的验证表明，最优储热集成可降低20年总成本73万欧元（16.5%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管储热在DHN中的效益已得到公认，但由于DHN在热源、生产和消费模式、网络热损失及燃料成本方面的多样性，确定最优储热容量仍具挑战性。现有的储热容量优化方法往往基于简化假设，难以同时考虑时变热生产成本、热需求和热损失的相互作用。

Method: 采用非线性、基于物理的数学优化方法，同时建模区域供热网络、热生产者（含CHP机组）和储热系统，以最小化总经济成本为目标，实现储热容量的最优配置。该方法明确考虑时变的热生产成本、热需求与热损失，且具有可扩展性，可自动化应用于不同配置的DHN。

Result: 在比利时一个有30个用户的第三代CHP-DHN上验证，规模超越了以往物基储热容量优化研究。最优储热集成使20年总成本从441万欧元降至368万欧元，减少73万欧元（16.5%）。其中热生产成本降低107万欧元（通过将热提取转移至低电价时段），储热投资为32.3万欧元。与常用简化储热容量方法相比，所提方法在确保可行设计、准确成本评估和最优储热容量方面具有明显优势。

Conclusion: 所提出的整体物基优化方法能够在大规模实际DHN中准确确定最优储热容量并评估其经济效益，优于常用的简化方法，为DHN储热规划提供了可靠的决策工具。

Abstract: Integrating heat storages in district heating networks (DHNs) supports managing dynamic characteristics such as heat-demand variations and changing energy prices, and the intermittency of renewable sources. A common application are DHNs with combined heat and power (CHP) units, where a storage allows shifting heat extraction to periods with favorable electricity prices. Although the benefits of heat storage in DHNs are well established, determining the optimal storage size remains challenging due to the diversity of DHNs in terms of heat sources, production and consumption patterns, network heat losses, and fuel costs. This paper presents a scalable, automated methodology for optimizing short-term heat storage in DHNs using mathematical optimization. The nonlinear, physics-based approach models the DHN, heat producers, and storages simultaneously to minimize total economic cost through optimal storage sizing, explicitly considering time-varying heat-production costs, heat demands, and heat losses. The methodology is demonstrated on a 3rd-generation CHP-DHN in Belgium with 30 consumers, exceeding the scale of previous physics-based storage-sizing studies. For this system, optimal storage integration reduces the 20-year cost by 730 k EUR (16.5%), from 4.41 M EUR to 3.68 M EUR. The reduction results from a 1.07 M EUR decrease in heat-production cost achieved by shifting heat extraction to periods of low electricity prices, while the storage investment amounts to 323 k EUR. A comparison to a commonly used simplified storage sizing method, which fails to identify the optimal storage size, demonstrates the advantage of the proposed holistic, physics-based optimization approach in ensuring feasible designs, accurate cost assessments, and optimal storage sizing.

</details>


### [88] [A Local-Linearly Convergent Algorithm for Nonconvex Equality-Constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12665)
*Frank E. Curtis,Lingjun Guo,Daniel P. Robinson*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文扩展了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的分析，证明了该算法在强二阶驻点附近具有局部线性收敛速率，并展示了其作为迭代高效子问题求解器在渐进采样策略中的应用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非凸等式约束优化问题广泛存在于实际应用中，而现有的Gradient-Eigenstep算法虽然具有迭代效率优势，但其收敛速率分析和在大规模样本平均问题中的应用潜力尚未被充分探索。

Method: 作者通过两个关键结果展开分析：(1) 理论分析证明当算法在强二阶驻点附近初始化、并采用足够小的步长参数和足够大的罚参数时，算法退化为对Fletcher增广拉格朗日函数的梯度下降，从而获得局部线性收敛速率；(2) 将上述结果应用于渐进采样策略，将Gradient-Eigenstep算法作为子问题求解器，以降低大规模样本平均优化问题的样本复杂度。

Result: 证明了Gradient-Eigenstep算法的局部线性收敛性质，并展示了其在渐进采样框架下能够获得优于直接求解全样本问题的最坏情况样本复杂度。

Conclusion: Gradient-Eigenstep算法不仅具有迭代高效性，还具备局部线性收敛保证，并且在处理大规模样本平均定义的约束优化问题时，通过渐进采样策略可以获得更优的样本复杂度。

Abstract: For solving nonconvex equality-constrained optimization problems, a recent Gradient-Eigenstep Algorithm by Goyens et al.~is an iteration-efficient approach, based on minimizing Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian function, for finding an approximate second-order stationary point from an arbitrary starting point. In this paper, the analysis of this algorithm is extended, offering a two-fold contribution. First, it is shown that a local-linear rate of convergence can be obtained by this method if it is initiated sufficiently close to a strong second-order stationary point and employs a sufficiently small step-size parameter and sufficiently large penalty parameter. In this case, the algorithm reduces to a gradient descent algorithm applied to minimize Fletcher's augmented Lagrangian. Second, as a particularly useful application of the first result, it is shown that the Gradient-Eigenstep algorithm can be used as an iteration-efficient subproblem solver in the context of a progressive sampling strategy for solving equality-constrained optimization problems when the objective and constraint functions are defined by large sample averages, ultimately offering an algorithm with an improved worst-case sample complexity when compared to an approach that solves a full-sample problem directly.

</details>


### [89] [Stochastic Mixed-Integer Optimization of Dynamic Electricity Tariffs with Consumer Protection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12688)
*Ananya Kale,Mohit Apte,Chhaya Gosavi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文利用55,566户Low Carbon London家庭的半小时级数据，研究了在保持收入接近平电价基线与限制账单增幅的约束下，日前居民分时电价所能实现的削峰效果。结果表明，分段保护型随机电价方案平均可将模拟峰值需求削减2.29%，且保护约束对削峰性能的损失很小，但分段平均账单上限无法约束家庭尾部风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日前居民电价必须在需求观测之前公布，激进的高电价虽能在仿真中削减峰值，却可能推高用户账单。因此需要量化"削峰能力"与"保护用户账单/维持收入"之间的权衡，即price-of-protection，以指导现实的电价设计。

Method: 使用Low Carbon London试验中5,566户家庭的半小时数据（1.678亿条有效读数），以标准电价为对照组进行准实验价格响应估计，构造bootstrap需求情景，采用HiGHS求解随机混合整数规划，对比分段账单上限保护与代表性家庭（尾部感知）变体的表现。

Result: 在全部73个合格留存测试日上，分段保护型随机电价平均削峰2.29%（95%日bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]），收入变化-2.26%，最差分段平均账单增幅2.48%。移除分段账单上限仅将削峰提升至2.37%，但最差分段账单增幅升至6.81%。家庭第95百分位账单增幅为8.61%（CVaR95为14.76%），表明分段平均上限无法约束家庭尾部。代表性家庭变体将家庭p95从8.61%降至7.05%，削峰仅从2.29%微降至2.28%。

Conclusion: 在该样本中，实质性的平均保护成本对削峰性能的损失很小（保护与无保护的削峰差距仅约0.08个百分点）；但分段平均账单上限无法充分约束家庭层级的尾部账单风险，需要结合尾部感知（代表性家庭）的保护策略来改善尾部表现，且几乎不牺牲平均削峰效果。

Abstract: Day-ahead residential tariffs must be posted before demand is observed. Aggressive high prices can cut peaks in simulation, but they can also raise customer bills. This paper measures how much peak reduction is lost when a tariff design problem is required to keep revenue near a flat-tariff baseline and to limit bill increases. Using half-hourly data from 5,566 Low Carbon London households (167.8 million validated readings), we estimate quasi-experimental price response against the standard-tariff comparison group, form bootstrap demand scenarios, and solve stochastic mixed-integer programs with HiGHS. On all 73 eligible held-out test days, a segment-protected stochastic tariff reduces simulated peak demand by 2.29% on average (95% day-bootstrap CI [2.11, 2.48]), with revenue change -2.26% and mean worst-segment bill increase 2.48%. Removing the segment bill cap raises peak reduction only to 2.37%, while the worst segment bill increase rises to 6.81%: in this sample, substantial average protection costs little peak-shaving performance. The same schedules leave a household 95th-percentile bill increase of 8.61% (CVaR95 14.76%), so segment-average caps do not bound household tails. We report the full price-of-protection frontier under consistent household simulation and compare segment protection with a representative-household (tail-aware) variant: the latter cuts household p95 from 8.61% to 7.05% while changing mean peak reduction only from 2.29% to 2.28%. All optimized outcomes are model-based counterfactuals under an opt-in trial; wholesale costs are unavailable, so we do not optimize profit.

</details>

Abstract: The necessary conditions for formulating optimal feedback control algorithms have been known for many years. Free parameters exist in the performance index in the form of state and control penalty and terminal state penalty matrices for tuning the performance of the optimally controlled system. The selection process is typically experimental and iterative. To generate the weight matrices for optimal feedback control algorithms, an optimization process is proposed. Typically, the optimization process for the weight matrices requires several numerical integration processes that are computationally expensive; this work overcomes the classical high computational cost by exploiting closed-form solutions for the time-varying Riccati matrix, state trajectories, state transition matrix, and the optimal performance index. Closed-form algebraic equations are used to generate all partial derivative calculations, and no numerical integration is required. The closed-form partial derivatives are used to generate analytic gradients for the optimization steps. The optimization strategy seeks to minimize the terminal state values for the feedback control problem. A numerical example is presented to demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed optimization algorithm. The resulting computational procedures are expected to be broadly useful for control theory applications in science and engineering.

Abstract: Multi-objective bilevel optimization has wide applications in the AI area such as automated learning and multi-task meta-learning. Although recently some works have been begun to study the multi-objective bilevel optimization, the proposed methods rely on the (strongly) convex lower level problems. In fact, these multi-objective bilevel learning problems are generally nonconvex, and particularly their lower level problems are nonconvex. To fill this gap, we propose a class of Multi-Objective Moreau Envelope based Hessian-free Algorithms (MOMEHA) to solve the multi-objective bilevel learning problems with nonconvex lower level. Specifically, our method uses the Moreau envelope to convert the original problem into a multi-objective single-level optimization with an envelope constraint. In particular, our method retains computational advantages of being single-loop and Hessian-free in the multi-objective setting by incorporating a smooth weighted Tchebycheff scalarization. Furthermore, we propose a momentum-based variant of MOMEHA (i.e., MB-MOMEHA) method to solve the stochastic multi-objective bilevel learning problems. In theory, we provide the convergence properties of our algorithms under both deterministic and stochastic setting. Some experiments on few-shot meta-learning and neural architecture search demonstrate that our methods outperform the existing approaches in Pareto front, validating its effectiveness and robustness.

</details>

Abstract: In this paper, we study the observability and controllability of a class of degenerate hyperbolic equations with a control region intersecting the degenerate set. Unlike the existing results that mainly deal with control regions separated from the degeneracy, we consider the case where the control region reaches the degenerate part. To handle the difficulty caused by the degeneracy, we introduce a shape-design-based approximation method based on shape design by approximating the degenerate equation with a family of uniformly hyperbolic equations. The proof relies on the spectral approximation of the associated operators, precise estimates for weak solutions, and the multiplier method. We first establish observability inequalities for the approximating equations with constants independent of the approximation parameter. Then, by passing to the degenerate limit, we obtain the observability inequality for the original degenerate equation.

Method: 在给定图拉普拉斯的前提下，提出差分凸正则化（DCR）图学习框架，通过正则化最大似然估计（MLE）近似拉普拉斯伪逆的谱作用。通过拉普拉斯正则化非负最小二乘（LR-NNLS）的对偶表示，将伪逆学习与实例特定推理解耦，并利用可微对偶引导学习方案实现高效的原问题解重构。同时建立了DCR算法的稳定性和不动点存在性的理论保证。

Abstract: Laplacian-regularized minimization is fundamental in signal processing and machine learning, but is limited by the dense and ill-conditioned nature of the graph Laplacian pseudoinverse. While the Laplacian itself is sparse, its pseudoinverse is dense and often ill-conditioned, rendering direct computation impractical at scale. Moreover, pseudoinverse learning is more challenging than Laplacian learning. To address this challenge, this paper considers the setting where the graph Laplacian is given and proposes a Difference-of-Convex Regularizer (DCR) graph learning framework that approximates the spectral action of the Laplacian pseudoinverse without direct inversion via regularized Maximum Likelihood Estimation (MLE). By reformulating Laplacian-Regularized Nonnegative Least Squares (LR-NNLS) through a dual representation, DCR decouples pseudoinverse learning from instance-specific inference and enables efficient primal solution reconstruction via a differentiable dual-guided learning scheme. We establish theoretical guarantees on stability and the existence of a unique fixed point for DCR algorithm. Numerical experiments demonstrate improved performance over convex solvers and graph filtering baselines and robust performance across diverse graph topologies.

</details>

Conclusion: 该惩罚型方法为逆最优控制中的双层优化问题提供了一种计算高效且具有收敛性保证的数值求解方案，其无需线搜索、下层问题单次求值和惩罚参数有界等特性使其在实际应用中具备显著优势。

Abstract: This paper is devoted to the introduction and analysis
  of a penalty-type method for the numerical treatment of
  a class of bilevel optimization problems arising from inverse optimal control.
  The algorithm is designed to compute stationary points of the associated relaxed value function reformulation.
  This is achieved by determining a sequence of stationary points associated with a sequence of surrogate problems
  where the relaxed value function constraint is penalized,
  where the updates of upper- and lower-level decision variables are decoupled,
  and where the penalty parameter is enlarged only in those iterations
  which do not come along with a sufficient improvement of some feasibility measure.
  The resulting method does not comprise any linesearch, the lower-level problem has to be evaluated just once per iteration,
  and the penalty parameter does not need to be driven to infinity.
  Nevertheless, subsequential convergence results are obtained under reasonable assumptions.
  Numerical experiments, where the relaxation parameter is also driven to zero, visualize effectiveness of the approach.

</details>

Abstract: Global warming, driven by anthropogenic carbon emissions with transboundary pollution characteristics and irreversible damage, poses an existential threat to human society. This paper develops a novel two-level Stackelberg game with mean field interaction of controls and common noise which integrates hierarchical decision-making under a state-reflected emission dynamics. A central regulator (leader) adjusts product prices to guide $n$ heterogeneous competing regions (followers) while enforcing a hard emission cap via a reflection mechanism that models emergency reductions through a local time process. We establish the existence of an approximate Stackelberg equilibrium and perform sensitivity analysis via Monte Carlo simulations.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 与有限维线性系统不同，无穷维线性系统的极点位于开左半平面并不能保证系统稳定。稳定性需要两个条件：(i)极点位于闭左半平面且(ii)无穷小生成元可对角化。因此需要研究离散线性集合系统在这些条件下的反馈镇定问题，给出静态线性反馈控制律存在的充要条件。

Result: 证明了序列(|b_n|)和(a_n/|b_n|)的指数衰减是静态线性反馈镇定闭环集合系统的必要条件；并且在关于期望极点的一定假设下，该条件也是充分的。

Abstract: We consider a countably infinite collection of linear, scalar control systems, where each system is represented by the pair $(a_n,b_n)$ with $a_n>0$ for $n\in \mathbb{N}$, and all systems are forced by a common scalar control input. We refer to this collection of systems as a discrete linear ensemble system. The ensemble system is feedback stabilizable if there exists a common feedback control input that asymptotically stabilizes every system simultaneously. Unlike finite-dimensional linear systems, an infinite-dimensional linear system is not guaranteed to be stable if its poles lie in the open left half-plane. Stability is guaranteed, however, if (i) its poles are contained in the closed left half-plane and (ii) its infinitesimal generator is diagonalizable. In this paper, we provide necessary and sufficient conditions for the existence of a static, linear feedback control law that ensures the closed-loop ensemble system satisfies (i) and (ii). In particular, we show that the exponential decay of the sequences $(|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ and $(a_n/|b_n|)_{n\in \mathbb{N}}$ is necessary and, under an assumption on the desired poles, sufficient.

</details>

Main category: math.OC

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Boussinesq系统（描述流体与热耦合的方程组）的可控性问题中，传统方法通常需要大量控制，而减少控制数量在物理实现上具有重要意义。核心困难在于速度方程（Stokes算子）与温度方程（Dirichlet Laplacian）的谱分解不同，缺乏共同的谱局部化结构，使得经典的逐分量谱论证无法直接应用。

Method: 1) 利用线性化系统的级联（cascade）结构克服耦合谱不匹配的困难；2) 提出混合可观测性估计：仅对Stokes分量进行谱局部化，温度分量不施加频率限制；3) 将该估计与高Stokes频率的耗散相结合，对线性化系统执行Lebeau-Robbiano迭代；4) 通过时间迭代论证将线性可控性估计迁移到非线性Boussinesq系统。

Result: 成功证明二维和三维Boussinesq系统的局部零能控性，且控制数量减少为温度方程加上N-2个速度分量（比完整控制少2个）。进一步，控制的小时间代价以C·exp(C/T)为上界，恢复了预期的抛物型数量级，同时保持控制数量的减少。

Conclusion: 该工作显著推进了Boussinesq系统可控性理论，证明了减少控制数量下的局部零能控性并保持最优级小时间代价，为流体-热耦合系统的控制提供了新的方法论（混合可观测性估计），可能推广到其他具有级联结构的耦合系统。

Abstract: In this paper, we prove the local null controllability of the Boussinesq system in dimensions two and three with a reduced number of localized controls. More precisely, the controls act on the temperature equation and on only $N-2$ components of the velocity equation. Our proof is based on a spectral approach in the spirit of the Lebeau--Robbiano method.
  The main difficulty comes from the coupled structure of the system. The velocity and temperature equations are governed by the Stokes operator and the Dirichlet Laplacian, respectively. Their natural spectral decompositions are different, and there is no common spectral localization naturally adapted to the coupled system. Therefore, the usual componentwise spectral argument cannot be applied directly. We show that the cascade structure of the linearized system makes it possible to overcome this difficulty. The main ingredient is a mixed observability estimate in which only the Stokes component is spectrally localized, while the heat component is treated without any frequency restriction. Combining this estimate with the dissipation of the high Stokes frequencies allows us to carry out a Lebeau--Robbiano iteration for the linearized system. The resulting linear controllability estimate is transferred to the nonlinear Boussinesq system through a time-iteration argument. As an important consequence, the controls have a small-time cost bounded by $C\exp(C/T)$, which recovers the expected parabolic order while preserving the reduced number of controls.

</details>


### [98] [Distribution Steering via Sliced Optimal Transport Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12828)
*Kaito Ito,Anqi Dong*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切片最优传输的有限时域分布控制框架，通过一维投影构造方向性终端条件，实现最小能量随机单方向控制器，并对方向平均得到确定性切片反馈律，可驱动系统状态分布在有限时间内从初始分布到达目标分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输为分布控制提供了几何方法，但通常需要全状态空间中的传输映射或耦合，计算复杂且不易实时实现。切片最优传输虽通过一维投影避免了全维构造，但其投影映射仅给出方向性位移，不能直接转化为可实现的状态反馈律。因此需要建立一种基于切片最优传输的可实现反馈控制框架。

Method: 在每个采样时刻，利用切片最优传输的一维投影映射定义方向性终端条件，通过最小能量实现得到随机单方向控制器；对所有投影方向求平均得到确定性切片反馈。对于单积分器系统，分析切片Wasserstein距离的单调性；对高斯端点分布，证明反馈是仿射的并保持高斯性。推广到线性系统，利用可达性归一化坐标和局部可控性Gramian实现精确有限步控制。

Result: 对于单积分器动力学，平均反馈使切片Wasserstein距离到目标非增；高斯端点情况下反馈为仿射且保持高斯性，可将均值和协方差驱动到指定终端值；识别出依赖于分布的增益可实现切片Wasserstein距离的线性衰减并显式刻画控制能量；随机控制器在采样周期趋零时收敛到平均切片流；并推广到一般线性可控系统。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了切片最优传输与可实现反馈控制之间的桥梁，提供了从投影最优传输映射到实际可执行随机/确定性控制器的完整构造，适用于单积分器及更一般的线性动力学系统，并通过数值实验验证了分布流动的效果。

Abstract: Distribution steering seeks feedback laws that drive the state law of a dynamical system between prescribed initial and terminal distributions. Optimal transport provides a natural geometric approach, but its implementation generally requires a transport map or coupling in the full state space. Sliced optimal transport avoids this full-dimensional construction through one-dimensional projections. Yet, the resulting projected maps specify only directional displacements and do not by themselves prescribe a realizable feedback law. To this end, we develop a finite-horizon control framework based on sliced optimal transport. At each sampling instant, a projected optimal transport map defines a directional terminal condition, whose minimum-energy realization yields a randomized single-direction controller. Averaging over projection directions gives a deterministic sliced feedback. For the single-integrator dynamics, the averaged feedback makes the sliced Wasserstein distance to the target non-increasing. For Gaussian endpoint laws, it is affine, preserves Gaussianity, and steers the mean and covariance to their prescribed terminal values. We further identify a law-dependent gain that yields linear decay of the sliced Wasserstein distance together with an explicit characterization of the control energy. We also prove that the randomized controller converges to the averaged sliced flow as the sampling period vanishes. Finally, we extend the construction to linear dynamical systems. Reachability-normalized coordinates allow instantaneous realization of the sliced velocity for uniformly fully actuated systems, while local controllability Gramians provide exact finite-step realization for general controllable systems. Numerical examples illustrate the resulting distributional flows.

</details>


### [99] [Distance-Based Formation Control with Prescribed Performance for Higher-Order Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12896)
*Janina Schaa,Maria Charitidou,Dimos Dimarogonas,Thomas Berger*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对具有未知非线性动力学（相对阶大于1）的多智能体系统，研究了具有规定瞬态性能的分布式距离基编队控制问题；引入虚拟领导者，在树图或最小刚性图等无向通信拓扑下设计了分布式无模型控制律，保证了编队和跟踪误差满足漏斗约束且所有闭环信号有界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体编队控制中，现有方法多假设系统动力学已知或相对阶为一，难以处理具有未知非线性动力学且相对阶大于一的实际系统；同时，如何保证编队形成过程中的瞬态性能（如收敛速度和超调量）也是一个尚未充分解决的挑战。

Method: 引入虚拟领导者的轨迹作为参考，针对树图拓扑下所有智能体跟踪虚拟领导者轨迹，以及在最小且无穷小刚性图拓扑下额外实现质心跟踪两种场景，分别设计分布式、无需模型信息的控制律，利用漏斗控制思想将编队误差和跟踪误差约束在规定瞬态性能包络内。

Result: 建立了编队误差和跟踪误差满足规定漏斗约束的理论保证，并证明了闭环系统中所有信号的有界性；数值仿真验证了所提控制律的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的分布式无模型控制律能够在不依赖系统动力学模型的情况下，对相对阶大于一的未知非线性多智能体系统实现具有规定瞬态性能的距离基编队与质心跟踪控制。

Abstract: We consider distributed distance-based formation control with prescribed transient performance for multi-agent systems modeled by unknown nonlinear dynamics of relative degree greater than one. We introduce a virtual leader whose trajectory is tracked with prescribed transient behavior by a designated subset of agents. The undirected communication graph is either a tree graph or a minimally and infinitesimally rigid graph. In the latter case, we further consider the objective of centroid tracking. In each setting, a distributed and model-free control law is developed, and we establish the satisfaction of the prescribed funnel constraints on the formation and tracking errors, and boundedness of all closed-loop signals. Numerical simulations illustrate the effectiveness of the proposed control laws.

</details>


### [100] [Optimizing Multi-Stakeholder Fairness in Computed Tomography Appointment Scheduling with Predicted Scan and Reporting Duration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13059)
*Ludovico Ambrosi,Chandra Bortolotto,Sara Cambiaghi,Luisa Carone,Davide Duma,Lorenzo Preda*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个"预测-优化"（predictive-prescriptive）框架，用于公平感知的CT随访排程。该框架先利用患者特异性机器学习模型预测检查和报告时长，再将预测结果嵌入多目标混合整数线性规划（MILP）模型中，同时最小化患者检查日期偏差并平衡放射科医生的报告工作量。在ε-约束框架内推导出支配性归约性质，大幅减少求取Pareto前沿所需的优化求解次数。基于真实急诊放射科数据的实验表明，仅1-2天的排程弹性即可在保持患者及时就医的同时显著改善医生工作量的公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT随访排程方法主要优化扫描仪利用率或患者等待时间，忽视了放射科医生的报告活动和多利益相关方之间的公平性平衡问题。需要一种能够同时考虑患者时间偏好和医生工作负载公平性的排程框架。

Method: 首先开发患者特异性ML模型（如XGBoost）分别预测检查时长和报告时长；然后将预测时长嵌入多目标MILP模型，采用字典序最小-最大公平准则平衡医生报告工作量，同时最小化与患者首选检查日期的偏差；在ε-约束框架内推导支配性归约性质以削减所需求解的优化问题数量；最后使用下游优化遗憾（downstream optimization regret）评估预测模型对排程决策的实际支持效果。

Result: 基于真实急诊放射科数据的计算实验表明：允许1-2天的排程弹性即可显著提升医生间工作负载公平性，同时不损害患者及时获取随访检查；提出的支配性归约策略在不影响Pareto前沿的前提下消除了绝大多数ε-约束评估；通过下游优化遗憾评估显示XGBoost在支持排程决策方面表现最佳，优于传统预测误差指标下表现更好的模型。

Conclusion: 该预测-优化框架为公平感知的CT随访排程提供了有效方案，兼顾了患者时间偏好与医生工作负载公平性两大目标。研究还揭示了传统预测指标（如预测误差）与下游决策质量之间的不一致性，强调应以优化遗憾作为评估预测模型价值的更合适指标。

Abstract: Scheduling follow-up Computed Tomography (CT) examinations requires balancing two competing objectives: assigning patients as close as possible to their recommended examination dates while ensuring an equitable distribution of radiologists' workload. Existing approaches optimize scanner utilization or patient waiting times, overlooking reporting activities and the need to balance fairness across multiple stakeholders. This paper proposes a predictive-prescriptive framework for fairness-aware follow-up CT scheduling. Patient-specific Machine Learning (ML) models are first developed to predict both examination and reporting durations. These predictions are then embedded into a multi-objective Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) model that simultaneously minimizes deviations from patients' preferred examination dates and balances radiologists' reporting workloads through a lexicographic min-max fairness criterion. We derive a dominance reduction property within an $ε$-constraint framework that substantially reduces the number of optimization problems required to generate the Pareto frontier. Computational experiments based on data from a real-world emergency radiology department show that allowing patients a scheduling flexibility of only one to two days is sufficient to substantially improve workload equity among radiologists while preserving timely access to follow-up examinations. The proposed dominance reduction strategy eliminates most $ε$-constraint evaluations without affecting the Pareto frontier. Finally, evaluating predictive models through downstream optimization regret demonstrates that XGBoost provides the most effective support for scheduling decisions, outperforming models that achieve lower prediction errors according to conventional predictive metrics.

</details>


### [101] [Adaptive Schauder Stochastic Mirror Descent in Banach Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13121)
*Jinhui Bai,Shuai Lu,Lei Shi*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了在无限维Banach空间中扩展随机镜像下降（SMD）算法的框架，用于求解仅通过采样获取随机梯度信息的一类风险泛函最小化问题，通过构造基于Schauder基的有限维子空间和新的正则化策略，实现了高效的显式求解与偏差-方差权衡，并证明了O(n^{-1/p1})的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的随机镜像下降方法主要局限于有限维或特定的Hilbert空间，而许多实际问题（如风险泛函最小化）自然存在于无限维Banach空间中，且该空间中缺乏均匀凸性，使得传统Bregman距离的选择和子问题求解变得困难。此外，随机梯度仅通过采样获取，给算法的可行性和收敛性分析带来挑战。

Method: （1）根据Banach空间的均匀凸性选择Bregman距离，对非均匀凸空间L_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)使用熵函数构造Bregman距离；（2）基于Schauder基构造随样本量n自适应变化的有限维子空间族；（3）在每次SMD迭代中，将子问题限制在对应有限维子空间上，并将随机梯度投影到有限维对偶空间，引入新的正则化策略；（4）提出算法复杂度为O(n^{1+θ})时间和O(n^θ)内存；开发新的分析框架证明收敛性，并扩展到逆问题。

Result: 算法在均匀凸性参数p1≥2决定下可实现O(n^{-1/p1})的收敛速率（忽略对数因子）；在误设定场景下（最小化器仅满足较弱正则性）算法仍能收敛到最小值；数值实验验证了算法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该工作首次系统地将SMD扩展至无限维Banach空间并处理了非均匀凸性情形，通过有限维子空间自适应投影和正则化策略实现了显式可解的子问题与偏差-方差权衡，为无限维空间中的随机优化提供了新范式，并在逆问题等实际场景中展现了应用潜力。

Abstract: In this paper, we extend stochastic mirror descent (SMD) to infinite-dimensional Banach spaces for solving a class of risk functional minimization problems, where stochastic gradient information is only available through sampling. We first choose the Bregman distance according to the uniform convexity properties of the Banach space. For the non-uniformly convex space $\mathcal{L}_{ρ_X}^1(Ω)$, we instead construct a Bregman distance induced by the entropy function. Based on a Schauder basis of the Banach space, we introduce a family of finite-dimensional subspaces that adapt to the sample size $n$. At each SMD iteration, we restrict the subproblem to the corresponding finite-dimensional subspace and project the stochastic gradient onto the associated finite-dimensional dual space, thereby introducing a new regularization strategy. This regularization strategy allows us to explicitly solve the SMD subproblem efficiently and to achieve a bias--variance trade-off. The algorithm requires $\mathcal{O}(n^{1+θ})$ time and $\mathcal{O}(n^θ)$ memory, where $θ>0$ can be chosen arbitrarily small when the minimizer has sufficient regularity. By developing a new analytical framework, we prove that the proposed algorithm achieves a convergence rate of $\mathcal{O}\left(n^{-1/p_1}\right)$ up to logarithmic factors, where $p_1\geq2$ is determined by the convexity properties of the underlying space. In the misspecified setting where the minimizer satisfies only weaker regularity conditions, we show that the proposed algorithm still converges to the minimum. We further extend the algorithm to inverse problems and validate its effectiveness in numerical experiments.

</details>


### [102] [Positive stabilization of a pure diffusion system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13203)
*Violaine Piengeon,Joseph J. Winkin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对具有边界控制和点观测的扩散系统的正镇定、正状态估计以及基于观测器的正镇定问题进行了完整的解析研究，包括最优设计的解析求解、观测器反馈镇定的新策略，以及任意阶空间离散化的数值实现与严格对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散系统在工程和物理应用中广泛存在，而正性（正值）约束在许多实际系统中至关重要。然而，针对具有边界控制和点观测的扩散系统，正镇定、正状态估计及观测器反馈镇定问题缺乏完整的解析研究，现有工作多为数值方法，缺乏可解析且便于实现的设计框架。

Method: 本文对三类核心问题（正镇定、正状态估计、基于观测器的正镇定）进行了完整的解析分析；对最优设计问题给出了解析解；提出了一种全新的基于观测器的反馈镇定策略；并对标称PDE系统进行任意阶空间离散化，在连续和离散两种设定下均推导出显式且易于实现的解，并与标称解进行了严格对比。

Result: 在连续和离散化设定下，所有三个问题的显式可执行解均被推导出来；最优设计问题获得了解析解；所提出的观测器反馈镇定策略被证明有效；离散化方案与标称系统的解之间进行了严格的比较。

Conclusion: 本文为边界控制扩散系统的正镇定与正估计问题提供了完整的解析理论框架和可实际部署的显式解，同时通过任意阶空间离散化保证了数值可实施性，架起了理论分析与工程实现之间的桥梁。

Abstract: This paper provides a complete analytical study of the positive stabilization, positive state estimation, and observer-based positive stabilization problems for a generic boundary control diffusion system with point observation. In particular, optimal design problems are solved analytically, and an original strategy for observer-based feedback stabilization is proposed. The three problems are also investigated for an arbitrary-order spatial discretization of the nominal PDE system enabling numerical implementation together with rigorous comparisons with the nominal solutions. In both the continuous and discretized settings, explicit and readily implementable solutions are derived.

</details>


### [103] [A stabilized sequential quadratic programming method for degenerate nonlinear optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13298)
*Yuya Yamakawa,Mamoru Oka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于黎曼流形上退化约束优化问题的稳定顺序二次规划（SQP）方法，在不需要任何约束规范假设的情况下，证明了生成的序列存在收敛到KKT点、近似KKT点或可行性问题驻点的聚点，并通过数值实验验证了方法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的黎曼SQP方法仅在约束规范成立的条件下才能保证全局收敛，对于退化问题（即约束规范失效的情形），其收敛行为无法得到保证。因此需要扩展稳定SQP框架到黎曼流形以处理退化约束优化问题。

Method: 将稳定SQP方法从欧氏空间推广到黎曼流形，针对具有等式和不等式约束的黎曼非线性规划问题（RNLP），在不假设任何约束规范的条件下设计算法，并证明其收敛性质。

Result: 证明了在不假设任何约束规范的情况下，所提算法生成的序列存在一个聚点，该聚点是KKT点、近似KKT（AKKT）点或关联可行性问题的驻点之一；数值实验确认了该方法对退化问题的有效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的稳定SQP方法成功地将稳定化技术从欧氏空间扩展到黎曼流形，能够在约束规范失效的退化情形下仍然保证算法的收敛性，弥补了现有黎曼SQP方法的不足。

Abstract: We propose a stabilized sequential quadratic programming (SQP) method for degenerate constrained optimization problems on Riemannian manifolds. The problem considered in this study is a Riemannian nonlinear programming problem (RNLP) with equality and inequality constraints, where classical constraint qualifications may fail. While existing Riemannian SQP methods guarantee global convergence only under constraint qualifications, their convergence behavior is not ensured for degenerate problems. To address this limitation, we extend the stabilized SQP framework from Euclidean spaces to Riemannian manifolds. Without assuming any constraint qualification, we prove that the generated sequence has an accumulation point that is a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) point, an approximate KKT (AKKT) point, or a stationary point of an associated feasibility problem. Finally, we conduct numerical experiments to confirm the effectiveness of the proposed method for degenerate problems.

</details>


### [104] [Distributed and Dynamic Hub Network Operation Planning in a Hyperconnected Less-Than-Truckload Operating System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13419)
*Tiankuo Zhang,Jihye Jung,Paria Nourmohammadi,Benoit Montreuil,Alan Erera,Sahrish Jaleel Shaikh*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本研究针对零担运输（LTL）行业效率低下的问题，基于物理互联网（PI）原则设计了一套多枢纽运营规划协议，支持多个区域枢纽网络间的协作，并通过仿真实验验证了该协议在提升系统效率、鲁棒性和可持续性方面的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LTL运输虽然比整车运输更具整合优势，但由于运营效率低下且中小型承运商网络碎片化，平均成本仍然显著较高。现有研究缺乏针对多枢纽网络间协作的运营规划协议，因此需要开发分布式、动态的物流枢纽网络系统来提升效率和可持续性。

Method: 基于物理互联网（PI）原则（模块化集装箱和开放资源共享），设计一套多枢纽运营规划协议。每个货物进入枢纽网络时定义预期到达时间和最晚离站时间，运营商分别使用所设计的协议规划枢纽内货物至目的地专用拖车的分配以及枢纽间拖车的调度时间表，并通过精心设计的枢纽网络间互联来实现合作。

Result: 通过多场景下的仿真实验评估了所提出协议在运营商多枢纽网络中的有效性，结果表明该协议能够实现合作结果并满足货物请求，提高了PI网络系统的实用性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该研究提升了基于物理互联网的物流网络系统的实用性和鲁棒性，支持枢纽网络间更大程度的合作，从而推动更高效和可持续的物流系统发展。

Abstract: The less-than-truckload (LTL) industry plays a vital role in enhancing the efficiency and sustainability of logistics systems, as LTL shipments offer greater consolidation opportunities than full-truckload shipments. Despite of this flexibility, the average cost of LTL shipments remains considerably higher due to less efficient operations and highly fragmented networks of small and medium-sized carriers. Building on our ongoing effort to develop a distributed and dynamic logistics hub network system grounded in the Physical Internet (PI) principles of modular containers and open resource sharing, this study focuses specifically on inter-hub and in-hub operations, with cooperation among multiple regional hub networks. Therefore, a shipment may traverse multiple cooperating hub networks. With respect to each hub network each shipment enters, it is defined by its expected arrival time at the entry hub and its latest arrival time at the exit hub. Based on the defined shipment information, we design a set of multi-hub operation planning protocols for distributed hub operators. In their operating networks, operators use our smartly designed protocol separately to plan in-hub shipments' assignments to destination-specific trailers and inter-hub trailers' dispatch schedules. With carefully designed interconnections between hub networks, the aggregated hub network system is well-positioned to achieve cooperative outcomes and fulfill shipment requests. We evaluate the effectiveness of the proposed protocol through a simulation-based experiment under multiple scenarios in an operator's multi-hub network. Overall, this research improves the practicality and robustness of PI-based networks and supports greater cooperation among hub networks toward more efficient and sustainable logistics systems.

</details>

### [105] [LoKiFormer: Locality-aware Attention with Decoupled Knowledge Memory for Efficient Large Language Model Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12419)
*Qiuwu Chen,Zimo Liu,Yuchen Li,Ying Sun,Yifan Zhang,Zhijie Qiu,Zeng You,Ryan Dong,Simeng Ma,Yaofo Chen,Mingkui Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出LoKiFormer，一种新型LLM架构，通过局部融合注意力（LFA）和知识记忆模块（KMM）分别解决自注意缺乏局部性归纳偏置和MoE知识存储与计算路径耦合的问题，预训练收敛速度比基线快1.33倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构在预训练阶段效率低下，主要源于两个限制：(1) 自注意机制缺乏对局部性的显式归纳偏置，导致对序列内部局部信息的冗余建模；(2) MoE将知识存储与计算路径隐式耦合，阻碍了对序列外部全局知识的灵活访问。这两大瓶颈促使研究者探索更高效的架构设计。

Method: LoKiFormer在标准decoder基础上增加两个专用模块：(1) LFA（局部融合注意力），在注意力中引入卷积融合操作，显式捕获局部模式，使注意力能作用于更具信息量的表示；(2) KMM（知识记忆模块），引入参数化的键值记忆，将全局知识显式存储在可寻址的槽位中，实现存储与计算的解耦，支持直接知识检索。

Result: 实验结果表明，LoKiFormer在预训练阶段的收敛速度比基线模型快1.33倍，验证了其在信息集成方面的有效性和高效性。

Conclusion: LoKiFormer通过显式解耦局部信息建模（LFA）和全局知识存储（KMM），实现了比现有LLM架构更高效的信息集成，在预训练效率上展现出显著优势。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable breakthroughs across various applications. However, their architectures remain inefficient in pretraining due to two main limitations: (i) self-attention lacks an explicit inductive bias for locality, leading to redundant modeling of sequence-internal local information; (ii) mixture-of-experts (MoE) implicitly couples knowledge storage with computational pathways, hindering flexible access to sequence-external global knowledge. To overcome these limitations, we propose LoKiFormer, a novel LLM architecture that augments the standard decoder with two dedicated modules: 1) Local Fusion Attention (LFA), which incorporates a convolutional fusion to attention, explicitly capturing local patterns and allowing the attention to operate on more informative representations; 2) Knowledge Memory Module (KMM), which introduces a parametric key-value memory that explicitly stores global knowledge in addressable slots, decoupling storage from computation and enabling direct knowledge retrieval. Together, these modules enable LoKiFormer to achieve more efficient and effective integration of information at both levels. Experimental results show that LoKiFormer converges 1.33x faster in pre-training than baseline models, underscoring its superiority over existing LLM architectures.

</details>


### [106] [Which Site, and When: A Free-Satellite-Data Test of Himalayan Glacial Lake Bursts, Landslides, and Ice Floods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12422)
*Matthew Kahn,Milan Arjel,Nirmala Adhikari,Mingmar Sherpa,James Pope*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究基于免费卫星数据（雷达干涉测量与气象数据），针对尼泊尔喜马拉雅冰川湖溃决、降雨触发滑坡及冰川小洪水三类灾害，提出并评估了预测模型。结果发现：前期天气能较好预测触发时机（ROC 0.73–0.83），地形对易感性的排序仅在匹配可比地点后真实系数为0.71–0.76，而小型洪水地形预测无显著效果；深度学习模型未超越梯度提升基线，湖泊灾害基线模型由三条规则的决策树复现。最终提供尼泊尔风险清单作为优先排序辅助工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期可行性研究虽表明形变指示湖体失稳、天气指示风险时机，但未提出预测模型。本研究旨在填补这一空白，基于免费数据构建并评估能预测"哪些地点易感、何时触发"的模型。

Method: 利用HMAGLOFDB中589个定年溃决事件及数千条编目滑坡数据，将每个事件与相似但未失败的地点配对；在空间交叉验证（按整块地图瓦片留出）下，将各模型对比强简单基线。针对三类灾害分别回答两个独立问题（易感性与触发时机），采用前期天气特征预测触发，地形特征排序易感性，并测试五种深度学习模型对比梯度提升基线。

Result: 前期天气模型触发时机预测ROC：大溃决0.73、滑坡0.83、小洪水0.82；地形排序在朴素评分为0.9左右但存在偏差，匹配可比邻近地点后真实值为0.76、0.71和0.54（后者不优于随机）。溃决信号在单一区域内部稳健，尼泊尔地区达0.89。五种深度学习模型未决定性地优于梯度提升基线，仅滑坡上有微弱的边际优势；湖泊灾害基线模型胜出，可由基于崎岖度和季风降雨的三规则决策树复现。

Conclusion: 免费卫星数据可有效预测触发时机且在区域内部具备良好泛化能力，地形对易感性的排序需谨慎解读（部分高估源于编目偏差）。复杂模型（深度学习）在此场景下无显著增益。最终成果为尼泊尔风险优先级清单，着重强调其作为排序辅助工具而非精确预测，并指出免费数据的应用边界。

Abstract: Two free satellite signals carry real information about glacial-lake outburst risk in the Nepal Himalaya: radar interferometry sees a moraine dam slowly sagging, and satellite weather marks the weeks when a primed lake is under stress. A companion feasibility study found that deformation indicates which lake is destabilizing and weather indicates when it is at risk, but proposed no predictive model. To address this gap, we propose and evaluate models that predict which site is susceptible and when a trigger arrives. We test three related hazards on free data alone: large moraine- and ice-dammed bursts, rainfall-triggered landslides, and smaller floods from ponds on and around a glacier. Each hazard gets two questions, never blended. Using 589 dated outbursts from HMAGLOFDB and several thousand catalogued landslides, we match each event against similar but unfailed sites, and hold every model to a strong simple baseline under spatial cross-validation that withholds whole map tiles, so no model succeeds by recognising a trained-on neighbourhood. Antecedent weather times the trigger at ROC 0.73 for big bursts, 0.83 for landslides, and 0.82 for small floods. Terrain ranks susceptibility only in part: scored naively it appears near 0.9, largely because catalogued failures cluster in wetter ranges; matched against comparable nearby sites the honest figures are 0.76, 0.71, and 0.54 (no better than chance). The burst signal holds within single regions, reaching 0.89 in Nepal alone. Five deep-learning models do not decisively beat a simple gradient-boosted baseline. Three score marginally higher on landslides, a hint too small to confirm. For the lake hazards the baseline wins outright, reproduced by a three-rule decision tree on ruggedness and monsoon rainfall. We close with a ranked Nepal watchlist, a prioritisation aid, not a prediction, and note where free data reaches its limits.

</details>


### [107] [MARCH: Scaling Recurrent Memory with Content-Routed State Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12435)
*Ming Zhang,Kaisen Yang,Shu Yu,Ermo Hua,Ning Ding,Xia Hu,Bowen Zhou,Chaochao Lu,Youbang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MARCH架构，通过定期缓存循环状态检查点作为"状态锚点"，并利用内容条件锚键进行注意力聚合，在不牺牲计算效率的前提下扩展状态空间模型的记忆容量，在长上下文检索与推理任务上优于多种线性注意力变体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer具有强大的长上下文检索能力，但训练时计算复杂度为二次方，推理时KV缓存随序列线性增长。循环模型（如状态空间模型）虽有高效解码，但固定大小的状态难以保留早期关联信息，在需要精确回忆的任务上表现不佳。

Method: MARCH定期将累积的循环状态检查点缓存为"状态锚点"，每个锚点关联一个紧凑的、基于内容的锚键。模型维护一个可随上下文长度增长的内存银行。在每个token处，MARCH生成一个锚查询，对所有因果可用的状态锚点进行注意力聚合，将聚合结果与当前状态融合。

Result: 在标准预训练后，MARCH在常识推理、LongBench以及上下文检索任务上持续优于多种线性注意力变体，证明内容路由的状态缓存能显著增强循环模型的长期记忆能力。

Conclusion: MARCH通过内容路由的状态锚点缓存机制，在保留循环模型原生高效计算路径的同时，有效扩展了记忆容量，实现了历史分辨率与内存成本之间的可控权衡，为长序列建模提供了新的架构方向。

Abstract: Transformers owe much of their strong long-context retrieval capability to a token-level memory that grows with context length. This flexibility, however, incurs a quadratic computation complexity during training and a key--value cache that grows linearly during autoregressive inference. Recurrent alternatives offer efficient decoding by compressing the entire history into a fixed-size state, but often underperform on recall-intensive tasks since earlier associations usually get overwritten by subsequent updates, and only the most recent contextual information is retained. In this paper, we introduce Memory-Anchor Routing across Context History (MARCH), a network architecture that effectively scales state-space models beyond a fixed-size dimension, while maintaining computational efficiency over long-sequences. MARCH periodically caches cumulative recurrent-state checkpoints as state anchors and associates each anchor with a compact, content-conditioned anchor key. This lets MARCH maintain a memory bank, which can grow as context length increases, providing a controllable trade-off between historical resolution and memory cost. At each token, MARCH produces an anchor query to attend all causally available state anchors, and the output is calculated as an attention-style aggregation over all historical anchors along the current state. We show that after standard pretraining, MARCH consistently outperforms multiple linear attention variants across commonsense reasoning, LongBench, and in-context retrieval. These results demonstrate that content-routed state caching substantially strengthens recurrent long-range memory while preserving its native computation path.

</details>


### [108] [Multi-AUV Ad-hoc network-based Target Tracking: A Value Gradient Guidance Multi-Agent Diffusion Reinforcement Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12436)
*Jiaao Ma,Chuan Lin,Guangjie Han,Shengchao Zhu,Qian Zhu,Ying Liu,Zhenyu Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出VGG-MADiffRL算法与MDCA三层架构，解决多AUV自组网在受限声学通信和海洋扰动下的协同目标追踪问题。VGG-MADiffRL将值梯度引入扩散策略的反向去噪过程，引导动作生成朝向更高期望回报，并采用孪生价值网络抑制过估计，实现更稳定的训练收敛和更高的追踪精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于多智能体强化学习（MARL）的方法在高维联合状态-动作建模和噪声敏感策略生成方面存在缺陷，导致训练不稳定和追踪性能退化。针对多AUV自组网在声学通信受限、拓扑动态变化和海洋扰动不确定等条件下的协同目标追踪需求，亟需一种更稳健、高效的解决方案。

Method: 1) 建模有声呐探测机制和洋流扰动的多AUV协同追踪MDP；2) 提出MDCA三层闭环控制架构（全局智能控制层、本地在线训练层、物理动作执行层）；3) 提出VGG-MADiffRL算法，基于扩散策略，在反向去噪过程中引入值梯度引导动作生成，采用孪生价值网络联合优化和软目标更新来缓解过估计和训练振荡。

Result: 实验结果显示VGG-MADiffRL在协同追踪场景中实现了更快的收敛速度、更高的追踪精度和更平滑的训练动态，验证了其在动态水下环境中的有效性和工程实用价值。

Conclusion: VGG-MADiffRL结合MDCA架构有效解决了现有MARL方法训练不稳定和性能退化的问题，为多AUV自组网协同目标追踪提供了可行的技术方案，在动态水下环境中展现出良好的鲁棒性和实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Multi-AUV ad-hoc network-based target tracking requires networked autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs) to cooperatively track maneuvering targets under constrained acoustic communication, dynamic topology, and uncertain ocean disturbances. Although multi-agent reinforcement learning (MARL) enables decentralized coordination through centralized training, existing methods suffer from high-dimensional joint state-action modeling, noise-sensitive policy generation, leading to unstable training and degraded tracking. To address these issues, we propose VGG-MADiffRL, a value-gradient-guided multi-agent diffusion RL algorithm, and MDCA, a diffusion?based hierarchical control architecture. Leveraging underwater mission characteristics, we model sonar detection mechanisms and ocean current disturbances, formulating cooperative tracking for multi-AUV ad-hoc networks as an MDP. The proposed MDCA constitutes a three-tier closed-loop control framework: a global intelligent control layer, a local online training layer, and a physical action execution layer. This structure enables synergistic optimization across task allocation, local decision processes, and execution feedback. Within MDCA, the local online training layer is the policy learning framework; VGG-MADiffRL builds on diffusion policies and incorporates value gradients to guide action generation in the reverse denoising process, steering the generated actions towards higher expected returns. It employs twin value networks with joint optimization and soft target updates to mitigate overestimation and training oscillations, promoting more stable convergence. Experimental results show that VGG-MADiffRL consistently achieves faster convergence, higher tracking accuracy, and smoother training dynamics in cooperative tracking scenarios, validating its effectiveness and practical engineering value in dynamic underwater settings.

</details>


### [109] [Unifying Generative Models with Path Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12438)
*Ramon Winterhalder*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将生成式建模统一表述为一个路径积分，其中基于流、扩散、变分和对抗的模型均作为单一"主作用量"的不同评估原则出现。其MSRJD形式将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，并开启了图论微扰理论。该展开产生了一个无需随机采样成本即可对确定性采样器进行单圈修正的方法，在可解及非线性漂移上验证有效（将53%的树级误差降至1.6%）。不完美的学习评分作为插入项出现，产生了响应加权的评分匹配目标，而对称等变漂移设计则成为具有EFT幂次计数的算子展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成模型（流、扩散、变分、对抗）在理论和算法上被分别对待，缺乏一个统一的数学框架来理解其内在联系和系统性地改进。传统扩散模型需要昂贵的高斯噪声扰动，且确定性采样器的误差缺乏系统的修正方案。

Method: 将生成建模重新表述为路径积分，利用Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de-Dominicis（MSRJD）形式化方法构造统一的主作用量。通过将自由概率流与相互作用概率流分离，引入图论微扰理论（费曼图展开），推导出确定性采样器的单圈修正项，并导出响应加权评分匹配目标及对称等变漂移的EFT算子展开。

Result: 在可解和非线性漂移的实验中，该方法将树级近似的53%误差降低到1.6%的单圈修正误差，且无需额外的随机采样成本。同时建立了不完美学习评分作为插入项的微扰处理框架，并给出了对称等变漂移设计的系统化算子展开方法。

Conclusion: 该工作为生成式建模提供了统一的场论视角，使不同生成模型家族可以在同一理论框架下比较和优化，并通过微扰展开为确定性采样器提供了低成本的系统误差修正方案，为后续更高阶修正和更复杂漂移设计奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We formulate generative modeling as a path integral in which flow-based, diffusion-based, variational, and adversarial models arise as different evaluation principles for a single master action. Its Martin-Siggia-Rose-Janssen-de~Dominicis (MSRJD) form separates free from interacting probability flows and opens them to diagrammatic perturbation theory. The expansion yields a one-loop correction to deterministic samplers at no stochastic-sampling cost, which we validate on solvable and nonlinear drifts, where it reduces a 53 % tree-level error to 1.6 %. Imperfect learned scores enter as insertions and yield a response-weighted score-matching objective, and symmetry-equivariant drift design becomes an operator expansion with EFT power counting.

</details>


### [110] [Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution: Architecture-Aligned Post-Hoc Explainability for Recurrent Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12441)
*Iyad Assaad Nekka,Hamida Seba,Khaled Walid Hidouci,Karima Amrouche*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出X-AddGraph，一个针对动态图边级异常检测模型AddGraph的严格事后可解释性框架，基于双时空归因（DSTA）机制，在不改变检测性能的前提下（Delta AUC=0）为异常边提供空间和时序维度的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态图上的深度学习异常检测器虽然准确率高，但完全不透明——当一条边被标记为异常时，分析师只得到一个分数而没有任何理由。在需要审计和信任的协作式、受监管的信息系统中，这种不透明性是站不住脚的。AddGraph作为基础性的GCN+GRU边级异常检测框架，至今没有任何形式的可解释性支持，这一空白亟待填补。

Method: 提出X-AddGraph框架，采用双时空归因（DSTA）机制，包含三个组件，每个组件对应AddGraph的一个架构模块：(1) 基于梯度的相关性归因，作用于当前邻接结构（空间维度）；(2) 直接读取推理期间已计算的上下文注意力权重（短期时间维度，零额外成本）；(3) 通过循环隐藏状态进行梯度回滚（长期时间维度）。检测器完全冻结，因此检测性能被精确保留。

Result: 在UCI Message基准上，训练好的AddGraph基线达到平均每快照AUC为0.8705，超过原始发表结果；X-AddGraph复现每个分数与基线完全一致。在四类边群体（高置信度真阳性、低置信度真阳性、假阳性、随机样本）上的评估表明，长期归因能识别出比随机选择携带显著更多反事实信号的历史快照（0.127 vs 0.074），这一能力是任何仅空间维度的解释器所不具备的。

Conclusion: X-AddGraph是首个为AddGraph提供可解释性的框架，在完全保持检测性能的前提下赋予分析师可审计的空间与时间归因解释，弥补了该类检测器在受监管信息系统中部署时的可解释性缺口，并提供完整实现以确保可复现性。

Abstract: Deep learning detectors for anomalies in dynamic graphs have reached strong accuracy, yet they remain opaque: when an edge is flagged, the analyst receives a score but no reason. This opacity is untenable in the cooperative, regulated information systems where such detectors are deployed, where automated decisions must be auditable and trustworthy. We address this gap for AddGraph, the foundational GCN+GRU framework for edge-level anomaly detection in dynamic graphs, which to our knowledge has never been equipped with any form of explainability. We present a strictly post-hoc explainability framework, X-AddGraph, built on a Dual Spatial-Temporal Attribution (DSTA) mechanism whose three components are each aligned with one of AddGraph's architectural modules: a gradient-based relevance attribution over the current adjacency structure (spatial), a direct reading of the contextual attention weights already computed during inference (short-term temporal, at zero additional cost), and a gradient rollback through the recurrent hidden states (long-term temporal). Because the detector is frozen, detection performance is preserved exactly (Delta AUC = 0, verified empirically to ten decimal places). On the UCI Message benchmark, our trained AddGraph baseline reaches an average per-snapshot AUC of 0.8705, exceeding the originally published result; X-AddGraph reproduces every score identically while adding explanations where none existed. Evaluated across four edge populations - confident true positives, low-confidence true positives, false positives, and random samples - the long-term attribution identifies historical snapshots carrying significantly more counterfactual signal than random selection (0.127 vs. 0.074), a capability that no spatially-blind explainer can provide. We release our implementation for full reproducibility.

</details>


### [111] [Personalized Scorer Modeling: A Learning-Based Framework for Deriving Robust Sleep Stage Labels from Multiple Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12446)
*Seyyed Ali Hoseini,Javad Baseri,Hamid Saadatfar,Edris Hoseini Gol,AmirHossein Eshghi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出一种基于学习的睡眠分期标注方法（LBH），通过混淆矩阵建模每位评分者的阶段特异性行为，聚合多专家信息构建更可靠的参考睡眠图，在DOD-H和DOD-O数据集上相比数据集标注和最佳评分者标注均获得一致的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 睡眠分期对睡眠障碍的诊断和管理至关重要，但大多数自动分期研究仅针对单一参考睡眠图进行模型评估，忽视了评分者间已知的变异性。因此，需要探索如何利用多评分数据集从多位专家的集体行为中构建更可靠的参考标注。

Method: 使用公开的DOD-H和DOD-O数据集，将EEG（C3-M2）和chin EMG信号分割为30秒epoch，每个模态提取30个特征，共60个特征（EEG+EMG）。提出学习型睡眠图（LBH），利用机器学习模型生成的混淆矩阵建模每位评分者的阶段特异性行为，经过列归一化后估计给定评分者标签下各真实睡眠阶段的概率，跨评分者聚合概率为每个epoch分配最终标签。使用随机森林、支持向量机和多层感知机分类器，在EEG-only和EEG+EMG两种设置下评估，并与数据集睡眠图（DH）和最佳评分者睡眠图（BSH）进行比较。

Result: LBH在所有设置下均一致提升整体性能。最佳结果来自随机森林配合EEG+EMG，在DOD-H上达到86.07%准确率、85.46%精确率和85.29% F1分数；在DOD-O上达到86.04%准确率、85.21%精确率和84.70% F1分数。

Conclusion: 个性化评分者建模可以在不丢弃任何单个专家信息的情况下改进参考睡眠图的构建，为多评分睡眠分期数据集提供了一种更可靠的标注聚合策略。

Abstract: Sleep stage classification is important for the diagnosis and management of sleep disorders, yet most automatic staging studies evaluate models against a single reference hypnogram despite known inter-scorer variability. This study investigates whether multi-scored datasets can be used to construct more reliable reference labels from the collective behavior of multiple experts. We use the publicly available DOD-H and DOD-O datasets. EEG (C3-M2) and chin EMG signals were segmented into 30-s epochs, and 30 features were extracted from each modality, yielding 60 features for EEG+EMG. We propose a learning-based hypnogram (LBH) that models the stage-specific behavior of each scorer using confusion matrices derived from machine-learning models. After column normalization, these matrices estimate the probability of each true sleep stage given each scorer's label; probabilities are aggregated across scorers to assign the final label for each epoch. LBH was evaluated with random forest, support vector machine, and multilayer perceptron classifiers under EEG-only and EEG+EMG settings, and compared with the dataset hypnogram (DH) and best-scorer hypnogram (BSH). LBH consistently improved overall performance. The best results were obtained with random forest and EEG+EMG, reaching 86.07% accuracy, 85.46% precision, and 85.29% F1-score on DOD-H, and 86.04% accuracy, 85.21% precision, and 84.70% F1-score on DOD-O. These findings suggest that personalized scorer modeling can improve reference hypnogram construction without discarding information from individual experts.

</details>


### [112] [Geometric and Behavioral Stratification in Transformer Residual Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12447)
*Nelson Guda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示训练后transformer模型的残差流中存在以"预测方向"（unembedding方向）为内容定义的特权锚点。围绕该锚点，残差流变化在几何和行为上呈现出按与预测距离分层的结构：靠近预测方向的窄接口集中了可读出的关键结构，远离预测方向的庞大补空间随模型规模扩展。这一分层在所有18个模型（稠密和MoE，7B-120B，基础版和指令微调版）中均成立。方差分析方法因预测方向几乎正交于主成分轴而只能部分恢复该组织。级联扰动实验表明，预测近端方向承载即时行为与任务框架，远端方向虽然在读出上弱对齐但具有因果和时间上的承载作用，行为由方向而非幅度驱动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究者希望理解transformer模型形成的特权坐标轴到底选择了什么样的方向——特别是回答"预测方向"是否构成一个内容定义的特权锚点，并阐明高维计算与线性读出如何共存这一根本问题。

Method: 以预测token的unembedding方向为参考锚点，在18个不同规模和架构的模型中测量残差流相对于该方向的几何统计与行为属性。采用锚定坐标系分析残差流的方差组织，并通过逐层级联（按与预测方向的距离分层）方向扰动实验测试各层级的因果重要性。

Result: 发现残差流按与预测方向的距离呈现陡峭的几何梯度：预测近端区域高度结构化且聚类相关提示，远端区域更平坦且对提示组反转区分。预测方向几乎正交于主方差轴，方差分析只能部分恢复该组织且差距随提示异质性增大。接口是窄但功能决定性：最接近预测方向的方向扰动导致立即发散和频繁任务框架切换，次近端方向延迟发散但保持框架；远端方向每方向弱对齐却在因果和时间上承载行为，且行为由方向而非幅度驱动。

Conclusion: 预测方向是与先前描述的坐标轴不同且内容定义的特权锚点。近端窄接口与远端平坦补空间的分层结构提供了高维计算与线性读出共存的一个几何解释。

Abstract: Trained transformer models develop privileged bases: coordinate axes whose statistics differ from the rest of the residual stream. But what kind of direction does such a basis select? We investigate the prediction direction, the unembedding direction of the token a model currently predicts, and find that it functions as a content-defined privileged anchor. Measured with respect to this anchor, residual-stream variation is geometrically and behaviorally stratified by proximity to the prediction.
  The stratification holds in all eighteen models tested (dense and mixture-of-experts, 7B-120B, base and instruction-tuned). A narrow, scale-invariant prediction interface concentrates readout-relevant structure, while the vast prediction-distal complement expands with model scale. Because the prediction direction sits nearly orthogonal to the principal variance axes, variance-based analyses recover this organization only partly, and the shortfall grows with prompt heterogeneity.
  Anchoring reveals a steep geometric gradient: prediction-proximal regions are highly structured and cluster related prompts, while the complement is flatter and anti-discriminates among prompt groups. The interface is a narrow slice but functionally decisive. Disrupting the variance directions closest to the prediction causes immediate divergence and frequent task-frame shifts; disrupting the next level down delays divergence and preserves framing. The complement is weakly readout-aligned per direction yet causally and temporally load-bearing, and behavior is driven by direction rather than magnitude.
  These results establish the prediction direction as a privileged anchor distinct from previously described coordinate axes, and give a geometric account of how high-dimensional computation coexists with linear readout.

</details>


### [113] [Exemplar-based objective classification of gust-induced loads across multiple flight conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12448)
*Paolo Olivucci,Kowshik Srivatsan,David E. Rival*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于机器学习的客观分类方法，通过编码大量实验观测数据并选择最小的高显著性样本集，为阵风诱导载荷提供可解释的分类标准。在3480个压力载荷测量数据（六种飞行姿态下的飞翼模型）上识别出九种跨姿态复现的基本响应类型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种客观的分类准则来组织多种飞行条件下的阵风诱导载荷复杂性，同时保持与传统基于粗参数（如飞行姿态）标注相当的直观可解释性。

Method: 通过机器学习表示编码大量实验观测数据，采用摘要/汇总程序选择最少数量的高显著性样本（exemplars），基于这些样本构建基于相似度的客观分类准则。

Result: 在6种飞行姿态、3480个随机阵风压力载荷测量数据上，识别出九种跨姿态复现的基本响应类型；对响应类型的瞬态分析揭示了底层流体力学的物理机理。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够以客观且可解释的方式分类阵风诱导载荷，选出的样本便于专家检查和开展更精细的实验，同时其分类结果能提供流体力学层面的物理洞察。

Abstract: Is it possible to find an objective classification criterion that organizes the complexity of gust-induced loads across many flight conditions? And one that remains as interpretable as a labelling based on coarse parameters, such as the flight attitude? Our approach encodes a large number of experimental observations through a machine-learned representation and applies a summarization procedure to select a minimal subset of highly significant exemplars. The exemplars provide a similarity-based objective classification criterion of all the observations, they can be more conveniently inspected by experts and can become subject of more refined experiments. We demonstrate the approach on a database of 3480 pressure-load measurements induced by random gusts on a flying-wing model across six flight attitudes. We find nine fundamental response types that recur across multiple attitudes; analysis of a type's transient response enables physical intuition into the underlying fluid mechanics.

</details>


### [114] [When Can You Trust Offline Evaluation of Equal-Cost Top-k Allocation? A Controlled, Reproducible Benchmark and Practitioner's Guide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12489)
*Binshuang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对有预算约束的组织选择处理对象时的离线策略评估（OPE）进行了系统基准测试。在五个数据集、两个已知效应扫描和六个估计器上，作者发现：弱重叠主要由logger与目标动作的"对齐程度"决定而非logger锐度本身；交叉拟合结果非参数不能修复优化器诅咒，需要诚实的策略级拆分；倾向性估计误差是最大的退化来源。所有数据均为公开数据，仅部分实验为合成或半合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织需要在预算约束下决定对谁进行干预，并希望在实际部署前了解某个目标规则（top-k确定性策略）能带来多少收益。离线策略评估（OPE）本应通过历史日志数据回答这一问题，但部署的规则是确定性的top-k策略，消除了一切动作平均，导致弱重叠问题直接影响估计量。因此需要系统性地评估不同估计器在此场景下的表现及失效机制。

Method: 作者在五个数据集和两个已知效应扫描上对六个OPE估计器进行基准测试，并用非模拟的配对参考验证机制。具体测试三类风险：（1）弱重叠风险：检验logger与目标动作对齐对支撑集的影响，使用有效样本量作为风险排序指标；（2）优化器诅咒：对比交叉拟合非参数与诚实的策略级数据拆分两种处理方式；（3）倾向性估计误差：比较得分倾向性（IPS）与双重稳健估计在倾向性估计不精确时的退化程度。实验中对倾向性进行0.02的下限约束，确保所有权重有界。

Result: （1）弱重叠由logger对目标动作的概率决定，而不是logger锐度；从目标自身得分构建的更锐利logger在测试范围内几乎不改变重叠，而动作级分歧会破坏它。有效样本量能在不同日志环境间排序风险，但在单个日志内排序候选时能力弱，且截断点不可迁移。（2）交叉拟合结果非参数无法修复重用偏差，甚至使其更糟；诚实的策略级拆分通过改变估计目标（估计学习过程的价值而非全样本策略的去偏）来避免重用问题。（3）倾向性估计误差是最大的退化源：越界估计对IPS的伤害超过其他任何压力测试，但几乎不影响双重稳健估计，甚至可能颠倒重叠诊断的结论。0.02的下限将两个调优混合估计器降为其未调优父体，最终只剩四个实践上不同的估计器。

Conclusion: 离线评估确定性top-k目标策略时，从业者应优先关注logger与目标动作的对齐程度而非logger锐度；需要诚实的策略级数据拆分来规避优化器诅咒，而非仅交叉拟合非参数；倾向性估计的精确性至关重要，双重稳健估计更能抵御这一风险。有效样本量作为诊断工具在跨环境比较中有效但在单一日志内作用有限。所有失败均在权重有界条件下发生，说明问题并非数值不稳定而是结构性偏差。

Abstract: Organizations decide whom to treat under a budget and want to know what a targeting rule would have earned before deploying it. Off-policy evaluation promises this from logged data, but the deployable rule is a deterministic top-k policy: it removes all averaging over actions, so weak overlap hits the estimate directly. We benchmark six estimators across five datasets and two known-effect sweeps, and validate the mechanisms against a non-simulated paired reference. First, weak overlap is governed by logger-target action alignment, not by logging sharpness alone: what governs support is the logger's probability of the target's actions. Sharpening a logger built from the target's own score barely moves overlap over the tested range; action-level disagreement collapses it. Effective sample size ranks this risk across logging environments, but is weak at ranking candidates within the single log a practitioner holds, and its cut point does not transfer. Second, the optimizer's curse is not fixed by cross-fitting the outcome nuisance. When the rule is fit on the data used to evaluate it, cross-fitting the nuisance alone leaves the reuse bias in place and makes it worse. Honest policy-level splitting avoids the reuse by targeting the learning procedure's value -- a change of estimand, not a de-biasing of the full-sample policy. Third, propensity-estimation error is the largest degradation we measure: an out-of-fold estimate hurts IPS more than any other stress we apply, leaves doubly-robust estimation almost unchanged, and can invert the overlap diagnostic itself. Logging is synthesized and propensities floored at 0.02, so every failure occurs with bounded weights; the floor also reduces the two tuned hybrids to their untuned parents, leaving four practically distinct estimators, and all exact-value surfaces are synthetic or semi-synthetic. We release the benchmark; public data only.

</details>


### [115] [Learning Under Treatment-Induced Label Indeterminacy with Expert Annotations of Counterfactual Outcomes: A Case Study in Neurological Prognostication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12477)
*Xiaobin Shen,Chloe Y. H. Huang,Jonathan Elmer,George H. Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对临床预后预测模型的新评估框架，该框架显式地将患者分为"确定案例"（可观测临床结局）和"不确定案例"（因治疗决策导致结局不可观测，由专家提供反事实推测）分别评估。研究基于2,497名心脏骤停后患者的队列数据，发现仅使用标准评估指标（如AUROC）会掩盖模型在不确定案例上的失败模式，且模型在确定案例上的准确性与对不确定案例标签的拟合之间存在显式权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床预测模型的开发通常假设患者的结局可被清晰观测，但在真实临床中，治疗决策可能使具有临床意义的结果永久不可观测。传统的统一评估方式无法识别模型在那些最需要预后支持的患者（即结局被治疗决策遮蔽的患者）上的失败模式，因此需要新的评估框架和模型设计来解决这一问题。

Method: 作者将患者分为两类：确定案例（结局可直接观测）和不确定案例（结局被治疗决策遮蔽，由独立临床专家提供反事实结局的推断标签）。提出了一个显式将两类案例分开评估的评估框架，并提出了一种简单预测模型，该模型同时利用确定案例和不确定案例的目标标签，允许在两者之间进行权衡。研究中比较了所提出的神经模型与多个表格基线模型。

Result: 在2,497名心脏骤停后患者的队列（含1,429名不确定案例）中，具有相似确定案例AUROC的不同模型在确定案例的Brier分数和对不确定案例的概率估计上存在显著差异。提升不确定案例标签对齐度通常以降低确定案例准确性为代价，揭示了标准评估所隐藏的显式权衡。

Conclusion: 当治疗决策决定临床上有意义的结果是否保持可观测时，传统的评估指标（如AUROC）可能遗漏那些最需要预后支持的患者身上的关键失败模式。该研究强调了在模型评估中显式处理不确定案例的必要性。

Abstract: Clinical prediction models are often developed as if the outcome of interest were cleanly observed for every patient. This assumption fails when treatment decisions make the clinically relevant outcome permanently unobservable. As a case study of this problem, we consider post-cardiac-arrest neurological prognostication using a cohort of 2,497 patients, including 1,429 patients whose outcomes were rendered indeterminate by treatment decisions. These patients with indeterminate outcomes were reviewed by independent clinical experts, who provided their guesses of counterfactual outcomes about what would have happened to the patients. We refer to these patients as uncertain cases. We also have patients for whom we observe their clinically relevant outcomes; we refer to these patients as certain cases. We propose a framework for evaluating prediction models that explicitly splits the evaluation between certain and uncertain cases. Here, we cannot easily evaluate both types of cases in a uniform manner as the available target labels differ. We then propose a simple prediction model that uses target labels from both certain and uncertain cases in a manner that allows us to trade off between them. Across the proposed neural model and a collection of tabular baselines, models with similar certain-case AUROC can nevertheless differ substantially in both certain-case Brier score and their probability estimates for uncertain cases. Improving alignment with target labels of uncertain cases for our proposed model generally comes at the cost of worse accuracy on certain cases, highlighting an explicit tradeoff that standard evaluation conceals. These results show that when treatment decisions determine whether clinically meaningful outcomes remain observable, conventional evaluation metrics can miss important failure modes in the very patients for whom prognostic support matters most.

</details>


### [116] [Interpretable Causal Discovery via Causal-Effect Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12640)
*Cixuan Zhang,Guy Van den Broeck,Benjie Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于罕见事件估计技术的贝叶斯因果发现方法，用于在给定因果效应约束等条件下推断因果图与参数的后验分布，解决了现有方法在小后验质量事件下失效的计算挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果发现的目标不仅是预测因果边，还包括解释和量化观察到的或假设的现象（如特别大的因果效应）。现有贝叶斯因果发现方法在目标事件后验质量很小时难以有效推断，因此需要一种能够在条件事件约束下进行因果推断的新方法。

Method: 将条件因果发现建模为贝叶斯推断问题，目标是对满足事件（如因果效应约束）的因果图和参数求后验。采用罕见事件估计技术，通过粒子群逐步向约束区域推进，同时维持近似条件后验的样本，从而在联合图-参数空间上进行高效推断。

Result: 在合成图上小规模和大规模实验均验证了方法的准确性；在Sachs蛋白质数据集上的案例研究表明，该方法能够提供通路级别的总结，辅助科学探索。

Conclusion: 该方法成功克服了条件因果发现中小后验质量事件带来的计算挑战，能够为因果图提供条件后验估计，并通过通路级摘要支持科学假设的探索与解释。

Abstract: Causal discovery aims to uncover the underlying causal relationships given data generated from a system. The goal, however, is not merely to predict causal edges given data, but also to be able to interpret and explain either observed or hypothesized phenomena, such as a particularly large causal effect. We consider this task of conditional causal discovery and cast it as a Bayesian inference problem, in which we target the posterior over causal graphs and parameters conditional on an event such as a causal-effect constraint. Unfortunately, this poses a computational challenge: existing approaches to Bayesian causal discovery struggle when the event has small posterior mass. To address this, we adapt rare-event estimation techniques to perform inference the joint graph-parameter space. Our method gradually drives a particle population toward the constrained region while maintaining samples that approximate the conditional posterior. Empirical evaluation on synthetic graphs validates the accuracy of our approach at small and large scales, and we show in a case study on the Sachs protein dataset how our method can be used to aid scientific exploration by providing pathway-level summaries.

</details>


### [117] [Training Under Challenge: Executable Certificates and Challenge-Closed Optimality for Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12655)
*Farhang Yeganegi,Arian Eamaz,Mojtaba Soltanalian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"Training Under Challenge"(TUC)框架——一种可执行证书方法，通过架构有效的挑战程序构建同一认证类中的完整替代方案并重新评估目标函数，从而对神经网络检查点给出经验全局最优性差距的下界。通过资源索引的挑战功率模量刻画通过认证的差距上界，并在平方损失下通过块下降算子实现可检查的覆盖。同时证明反向前沿：没有覆盖机制时，一阶ReLU训练器可达到无限多个精确条件头最优解却收敛到非全局点。在通道门控ResNet-18蒸馏任务中，8个内部挑战覆盖全部240个审计输出方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平坦训练曲线无法揭示网络是否达到全局最优、陷入局部陷阱、受表征限制或与训练器不匹配。现有方法缺乏一种可执行、可复现的机制来定量评估神经网络检查点与全局最优之间的差距。

Method: 提出TUC可执行证书框架：(1) 预声明、架构有效的程序构建同认证类的完整替代候选并重评估目标函数；(2) 任何更低值候选作为可重放的见证，给出全局最优性差距的下界；(3) 定义资源索引的挑战功率模量刻画最大兼容差距；(4) 对平方损失使用块下降算子使覆盖可检查；(5) 推导出均匀和实现残差界限，并证明无覆盖时一阶ReLU训练器的收敛反例。

Result: 在通道门控ResNet-18蒸馏问题上，8个内部挑战覆盖所有240个审计输出方向，实现残差界限位于真实差距的1.74--3.02倍以内。配对预测性证书能区分解码器利用不足与表征不足，量化去噪实验展示了诊断、修复与当前状态重认证。

Conclusion: TUC框架提供了一种可证明的、可执行的机制来量化神经网络的全局最优性差距，同时明确指出通过有限测试套件只能获得套件相对结论，全局差距结论需要单独论证的覆盖机制，这为深度学习的优化诊断提供了更严格的理论基础。

Abstract: A flat training curve does not reveal whether a neural network has reached a global optimum, is locally trapped, is representation-limited, or is mismatched to its trainer. We introduce Training Under Challenge, an executable-certificate framework in which predeclared, architecture-valid procedures construct complete alternatives in the same certified class and reevaluate the same objective. Any lower-valued candidate is a replayable witness that lower-bounds the checkpoint's empirical global-optimality gap. Passing a finite suite is only suite-relative; global-gap conclusions require a separately justified coverage mechanism. We define a resource-indexed challenge-power modulus that characterizes the largest gap compatible with passage. For squared loss, current block-decrease operators make coverage checkable and yield uniform and realized-residual bounds. We prove the converse frontier: without coverage, a first-order ReLU trainer can reach infinitely many exact conditional head optima while converging to a non-global point. On a channel-gated ResNet-18 distillation problem with known optimum, eight internal challenges cover all 240 audited output directions, and realized-residual bounds lie within factors of 1.74--3.02 of the true gap. Paired predictive certificates separate decoder under-use from representation insufficiency, while quantized-denoising studies demonstrate diagnosis, repair, and current-state recertification.

</details>


### [118] [Exploring Oversmoothing with Householder Matrices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12514)
*Bhaskar Karol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Householder图神经网络（HouseGNN），通过使用邻居聚合消息估计反射方向，并用Householder反射器配合GroupSort更新节点嵌入，构建逐层正交的图神经网络层，以避免深度GNN常见的过平滑问题。理论证明该模型在每层保持节点欧氏范数、对消息具有尺度与符号不变性，并能通过节点级正交算子间的错配改变节点间距离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度图神经网络存在过平滑问题：随着网络层数增加，归一化图传播算子的反复直接作用于隐藏表示会导致节点表征向低信息子空间逐渐塌缩，丧失区分度。传统GCN直接以聚合消息更新隐藏状态，无法避免这一问题。

Method: HouseGNN不直接使用聚合邻居消息更新隐藏状态，而是仅用消息估计一个反射方向；随后通过Householder反射器加上GroupSort激活函数更新节点嵌入，构成逐片正交（piecewise orthogonal）的层。该正交层在每一节点和每一深度都保持欧氏范数。

Result: 论文证明了三个核心性质：(i) 每一内部层都保持逐节点的欧氏范数；(ii) Householder反射器在消息的尺度与符号上具有不变性；(iii) 节点间的成对距离可以通过节点级正交算子之间的错配而发生变化。

Conclusion: HouseGNN通过引入Householder反射与GroupSort构造正交层，从理论上保证了深度传播过程中的范数保持，同时允许节点间距离变化，从而有效缓解过平滑问题，为深层GNN的设计提供了新的理论支撑和方法框架。

Abstract: Deep graph neural networks(GNNs) suffer from oversmoothing- a progressive collapse of node representation towards a low information subspace as network depth increases because the normalized graph propagation operator is repeatedly applied directly to the hidden representations. In this work we study Householder Graph Neural Network (HouseGNN). Rather than updating the hidden state like standard GCN, HouseGNN uses the aggregated neighbourhood message solely to estimate a reflection direction; the node embedding is then updated by a Householder reflector followed by GroupSort, yielding a piecewise orthogonal layer that preserves Euclidean norm at every node and at every depth. We prove three core properties: (i) every internal layer preserves the node-wise Euclidean norm; (ii) the Householder reflector is scale scale and sign-invariant in the message; and (iii) pairwise distance between nodes can change through mismatch between node-wise orthogonal operators.

</details>


### [119] [GENADA: efficient generative time series adversarial attack framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12535)
*Michael Baronov,Denis Vorobev,Margarita Rusanova,Petr Sokerin,Alexey Zaytsev*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GENADA（生成式对抗攻击），通过学习生成模型在前向传播中直接生成欺骗性扰动，替代传统迭代梯度攻击中重复反向传播的高计算开销。在时间序列领域多个模型和数据集上，GENADA在保持与强基线相当的攻击质量的同时，显著降低了生成扰动的时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在时间序列分析（如医疗、金融、能源、环境监测）中广泛应用，但易受对抗攻击。传统基于梯度的攻击方法（迭代一阶方法）需要在多次迭代中反复反向传播计算输入梯度，计算负担沉重，生成攻击扰动的效率低下。

Method: 提出GENADA，训练一个生成模型直接在单次前向传播中学习产生欺骗性扰动，避免迭代式反向传播。提供单步和迭代两种生成攻击方案变体，并给出了相应的训练流程。

Result: 在时间序列领域的多个神经网络模型和数据集（包括可控低维设置）上进行验证，GENADA在攻击质量上与强基线方法相当，但在推理阶段生成扰动所需的时间更少。

Conclusion: 生成式对抗攻击能够以更高效的计算方式实现与强基线相当的攻击效果，为高效的时间序列对抗攻击提供了一条新路径。

Abstract: Deep learning models are widely used for time series analysis in domains such as healthcare, finance, energy systems, and environmental monitoring. However, these models remain vulnerable to adversarial attacks, where small input perturbations cause severe degradation in predictive performance. Commonly used gradient-based attacks, iterative first-order methods, are computationally burdensome, as they repeatedly backpropagate through the victim model to compute input gradients during a number of iterative refinement steps. We propose a GENerative ADversarial Attack (GENADA) that learns a generative model to produce deceptive perturbations directly in a single forward pass and a procedure to train it. Variants include single-step and iterative generative attack schemes. The validation considers attacks on several neural models and datasets in the time-series domain, a controlled, low-dimensional setting. Empirically, GENADA achieves comparable attack quality to strong baselines while requiring less time to generate perturbations during inference.

</details>


### [120] [Scaling Automatic Research Agents via World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12564)
*Xiyuan Yang,Sheikh Sarwar,Jingru Cheng,Zhan Shi,Duanshun Li,Huiyuan Chen,Haiyang Zhang,Chenlei Guo,Jingrui He,Zhenyu Liao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出世界模型强化学习（WMRL）方法，通过用世界模型替代环境执行来解决AutoResearch代理强化学习训练中环境执行成为瓶颈的问题，并设计在线去偏和逆方差降噪两种缓解措施来克服世界模型的不完美性，实现3-4倍训练加速并超越标准RL基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动研究（AutoResearch）代理是AI长期的研究方向之一，现代LLM展示了独立实现解决方案并从执行结果中学习的能力，其中后训练（特别是强化学习）发挥核心作用。然而，作者发现当扩展这些代理的RL训练时存在根本性矛盾：代理生成和环境执行两个组件以截然不同的方式扩展——所有生成通过批处理共享计算，而每次执行独占沙箱和真实机器时间。因此环境执行主导训练成本并随着轨迹增长成为瓶颈，迫切需要解决这一规模化瓶颈问题。

Method: WMRL用世界模型替代环境执行以消除瓶颈。由于世界模型可能不完美，其奖励会被偏差和噪声污染，作者进一步设计了两种缓解策略：在线去偏（Online Debiasing）用以抵消偏差，以及逆方差降噪（Inverse-Variance Denoising）用以抑制噪声。在理论上证明了这两种缓解策略均严格改善收敛保证。

Result: WMRL在不同任务和不同代理规模下实现3-4倍训练加速，同时性能超过标准RL基线。后训练的4B和9B代理在保留基准上优于更大的48B和120B开放权重代理。此外，WMRL还成功迁移到具身VLA策略的后训练中，展示了方法的泛化能力。

Conclusion: WMRL通过世界模型替代环境执行成功解决了AutoResearch代理RL训练中环境执行成为瓶颈的问题，使得训练速度提升3-4倍且性能更优。理论上证明了其两种缓解策略的收敛优势，实证上验证了从AutoResearch到具身VLA策略的广泛适用性，表明该方法具有很好的通用性和扩展潜力。

Abstract: Automating empirical research is a long-standing direction of AI. Recent automatic research (AutoResearch) agents bring this goal within reach, as modern LLMs show the capability to independently implement solutions and learn from the execution outcomes. Behind these gains, post-training (especially RL) plays a central role. In this paper, we identify a fundamental tension when scaling RL for these agents: the two components of every AutoResearch trajectory (agent generation and environment execution) scale in very different manners, since all generation shares compute through batching, while each execution occupies its exclusive sandbox and real machine time. As a result, the environment execution dominates the training cost and becomes the bottleneck as trajectories grow. To resolve this tension, we propose World Model RL (WMRL), which replaces environment execution with a world model to remove this bottleneck. Additionally, the world model can be imperfect, as its rewards are corrupted by bias and noise. Therefore, we further equip WMRL with two mitigations, Online Debiasing and Inverse-Variance Denoising, which offset the bias and suppress the noise respectively. Theoretically, we prove that both mitigations of WMRL strictly improve the convergence guarantee. Empirically, WMRL accelerates training by 3-4x on various tasks at different agent scales, while exceeding the performance of standard RL baselines. Moreover, our post-trained 4B and 9B agents outperform much larger open-weight agents of 48B and 120B on held-out benchmarks. Beyond AutoResearch, WMRL also transfers to post-training embodied VLA policies, which demonstrates the generalizability of our method.

</details>


### [121] [Prof-K: Probabilistic One-Pass Filtering for Efficient Top-k Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12573)
*Tadeusz Dziarmaga,Witold Sikora,Łukasz Struski,Jacek Tabor,Marcin Mazur*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Prof-K，一种分布无关的top-k选择算法，通过单遍过滤流程（随机采样估计自适应阈值+紧凑缓冲区+精确top-k），提供至少1-ε的概率正确性保证，在PyTorch topk和RadiK基础上实现1.5x-10x加速，并成功应用于BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Top-k选择是数据库、信息检索、信号处理和现代机器学习（如稀疏激活、注意力剪枝）中的基础计算原语。随着数据规模增长，现有方法面临效率瓶颈：精确方法计算和内存开销高，近似方法依赖脆弱启发式且无法应对对抗性或重尾输入。

Method: Prof-K采用单遍过滤流程：首先用少量随机样本估计自适应阈值，然后将N个输入元素流式写入紧凑缓冲区，最后在缓冲区上执行精确top-k例程。算法提供概率正确性保证（至少1-ε），推导了缓冲区大小的高概率界和近似最优采样尺寸，通过放宽召回目标实现精度-速度权衡。

Result: 实验表明Prof-K相较于高度优化的PyTorch topk和RadiK实现获得1.5x-10x加速，在大规模、小到中等k值场景提升最显著。保证独立于输入分布，对对抗性设置具有鲁棒性。同时展示了其在BatchTopK稀疏自编码器训练中的实际效益（top-k选择占据训练成本显著部分）。

Conclusion: Prof-K通过分布无关的概率保证和高效的单遍过滤设计，为大规模top-k选择提供了兼具正确性、可扩展性和鲁棒性的解决方案，并展示了在深度学习训练流程中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Top-k selection is a fundamental computational primitive with applications spanning databases, information retrieval, signal processing, and modern machine learning workloads, including sparse activations and attention pruning. As data sizes grow, existing approaches become inefficient: exact methods incur high memory and compute overhead, while approximate methods often rely on brittle heuristics that degrade under adversarial or heavy-tailed inputs. In this paper, we introduce Prof-K, a fast, scalable, and distribution-agnostic top-k algorithm with probabilistic correctness guarantees. Prof-K performs a single-pass filtering procedure: a small random sample estimates an adaptive threshold, the N input elements are streamed once into a compact buffer, and an exact top-k routine on this buffer recovers the true top-k elements with probability at least 1 - $ε$, where $ε$ > 0 is user specified. We derive high-probability guarantees for correctness and buffer size, together with an approximately optimal sample size that minimizes overhead as a function of N and k. Empirically, Prof-K achieves 1.5x-10x speedups over the highly optimized PyTorch topk and recent RadiK implementations, with the largest gains in the large-scale, small-to-moderate-k regime where prior methods struggle most. Unlike previous approaches, these guarantees hold independently of the input distribution, ensuring robustness to adversarial settings. By relaxing the recall target (e.g., recovering 95% of the true top-k values), Prof-K additionally provides a principled accuracy-speed trade-off. We further demonstrate its impact on training BatchTopK Sparse Autoencoders (SAEs), where top-k selection constitutes a significant portion of the training cost.

</details>


### [122] [CardioState-JEPA: Delay-Aware Cross-Modal Learning of a Shared Cardiac Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12944)
