Our model, dubbed \emph{Symmetry-breaking Crystal Diffusion} (SbCD), introduces a principled approach to explicitly incorporate inter-space-group transitions into the generative process. In de novo generation experiments on MP20 and MPTS-52, SbCD outperforms its symmetry-preserving counterpart by a substantial margin, offering a promising perspective for generative modeling of crystalline materials.

</details>


### [188] [Doubly Robust Estimation of Causal Effect on CVR with Targeted Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13461)
*Jiayi Dan,Bo Li,Lu Deng,Yong Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从半参数理论视角重新审视点击后转化率（CVR）的因果效应估计问题，提出了一个针对链式结构结果的双重稳健因果效应估计器，具备更快的收敛速度，并通过目标正则化框架提升数值稳定性与实践适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CVR是电商和广告中的关键指标，估计其因果效应具有重要实际意义。然而，直接将现有因果推断方法应用于点击样本会引入样本选择偏差和方差增大的问题。已有的"理想损失"方法虽然在全样本上实现了损失的无偏估计，但损失的无偏性并不能保证最终估计器的无偏性，因此需要从根本上重新审视该问题。

Method: 从半参数理论出发，针对CVR这类链式结构结果开发了一个双重稳健因果效应估计器，详细推导了其理论性质，证明其收敛速度优于nuisance参数估计，对包括神经网络在内的灵活非参数估计器更具鲁棒性；在此基础上设计了基于目标正则化的框架以提升数值稳定性和实际可用性。

Result: 在合成数据和真实数据上的大量实验验证了方法的有效性和鲁棒性，并发现简单地将损失去偏与标准因果估计器结合的效果劣于所提出方法，突出了为CVR式目标量身定制且具有坚实理论保证的新估计器的必要性。

Conclusion: 该方法通过半参数理论和目标正则化的结合，为链式结构结果的因果效应估计提供了更稳健、收敛更快的解决方案，填补了理想损失方法在估计器无偏性方面的理论空白。

Abstract: Post-click conversion rate (CVR) is a key metric in various scenarios including e-commerce and advertising, reflecting the efficiency and user experience in the second stage of the conversion process. Estimating the causal effect on CVR is therefore of great practical importance. However, directly applying existing causal inference methods to clicked samples introduces sample selection bias and increased variance due to the exclusion of non-click data. Recent studies on CVR prediction introduce "ideal loss", which optimizes model parameters using an unbiased estimate of the loss over the full sample. Nevertheless, there is no guarantee that unbiasedness of the loss implies unbiasedness of the final estimator.
  We revisit this challenge from the perspective of semiparametric theory. Specifically, we develop a new doubly robust causal effect estimator for chain-structured outcomes such as CVR, and derive its theoretical properties in detail. It achieves a faster convergence rate compared to nuisance parameters estimation and is therefore more robust when using flexible nonparametric estimators, including neural networks. Based on these theoretical findings, we further design a framework based on targeted regularization to improve numerical stability and practical applicability.
  Extensive experiments on synthetic and real-world data demonstrate the effectiveness and robustness of our method. In addition, we find that naively combining loss debiasing with standard causal estimators underperforms our method, highlighting the necessity of developing the new estimator tailored to this CVR-style objective with solid theoretical guarantees.

</details>


### [189] [Concept Drift Detection and Adaptive Retraining of Malware Classification Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13465)
*Christofer Washington Berruz Chungata,Martin Jurecek,Katerina Potika,William B. Andreopoulos,Mark Stamp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了机器学习模型在恶意软件分类中因概念漂移导致的性能退化问题，提出了一种基于单类支持向量机（OCSVM）的新颖概念漂移检测方法，并与Mini-batch K-Means和最大均值差异（MMD）方法进行对比。实验覆盖四种学习模型（MLP、随机森林、SVM、XGBoost）和三种重训练场景（静态、周期性、漂移感知），发现所有漂移检测技术在分类准确率上接近周期性重训练，但显著提高了训练效率；其中OCSVM方法总体表现最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 攻击者不断修改恶意软件导致概念漂移现象频发，这使得基于机器学习构建的恶意软件检测/分类模型性能随时间的推移而严重退化。现有的周期性重训练策略虽然有效但计算成本高昂，因此需要一种能够自动、准确检测概念漂移的技术，从而实现仅在必要时进行模型重训练的"漂移感知"策略，在保持分类精度的同时大幅提升训练效率。

Method: 本文提出了一种基于单类支持向量机（OCSVM）的概念漂移自动检测方法，并与已研究的Mini-batch K-Means（MK-Means）和统计方法最大均值差异（MMD）进行对比。实验使用四种学习模型（多层感知机、随机森林、支持向量机、极限梯度提升），在三种场景下评估：静态场景（不重训练）、周期性场景（持续重训练）和漂移感知场景（仅在检测到漂移时重训练）。通过Pareto Front分析评估漂移感知场景下准确率与训练效率之间的权衡。

Result: 实验结果表明，三种概念漂移检测技术在分类准确率方面均能达到与周期性重训练相当的水平，同时在需要重训练的模型数量上显著减少，提供了更高的效率。基于OCSVM的漂移感知重训练方法在总体上优于MK-Means和MMD方法。

Conclusion: 研究提供了有力证据表明，在恶意软件分类模型中能够准确实时地检测概念漂移，且所提出的OCSVM方法能够在保持高分类准确率的同时显著降低重训练成本，是一种实用且高效的概念漂移检测解决方案。

Abstract: Concept drift refers to changes over time in the statistical properties of data, as compared to the data that was used to train a learning model. Machine learning models for malware detection or classification are particularly susceptible to performance degradation caused by concept drift, as attackers constantly modify existing malware. In this chapter, we analyze two machine learning-based approaches to automated concept drift detection-a novel approach based on One-Class Support Vector Machines (OCSVM) and a previously-studied technique based on Minibatch K-Means (MK-Means). For comparison we also consider Maximum Mean Discrepancy (MMD), a statistical technique for detecting changes in multidimensional data. We conduct an extensive series of experiments comparing the effectiveness of four learning models, namely, Multilayer Perceptron, Random Forest, Support Vector Machines, and eXtreme Gradient Boosting. For each of these models, we consider three distinct scenarios: A static scenario where no model retraining occurs, a periodic scenario where models are constantly retrained irrespective of concept drift, and a drift-aware scenario where models are only retrained when concept drift is detected. Under the drift-aware scenario, we analyze the tradeoff between accuracy and training efficiency using Pareto Front analysis. We find that all three concept drift detection techniques achieve classification accuracy comparable to periodic retraining, while offering substantially greater efficiency in terms of the number of models that must be retrained. In addition, drift-aware retraining based on our OCSVM technique generally outperforms the MK-Means and MMD approaches. Overall, these results provide strong evidence that we can accurately detect concept drift in malware classification models.

</details>


### [190] [Active-Trace Complexity Bounds for Moreau--Yosida Unadjusted Langevin Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13467)
*Yuchen Xin,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究非光滑复合目标分布下Moreau-Yosida非调节Langevin算法（MYULA）的迭代复杂度。作者证明MYULA的主导离散化误差由参考活性迹B_ref控制，而非全局曲率界d/λ。对于分段线性、lasso型、分组和全变差等结构化惩罚，曲率-管估计使B_ref与λ无关，从而获得Õ(ε^-2)的精度依赖，优于通用估计给出的Õ(ε^-3)。同时给出Moreau偏差界，确保端到端收敛保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对非光滑复合目标分布的Langevin采样算法，现有分析通常使用全局曲率界d/λ来刻画MYULA的离散化误差，这导致保守的迭代复杂度估计。作者希望通过更精细的活性迹（active trace）概念，揭示MYULA在实际结构化惩罚问题（如lasso、TV）中的真实收敛行为，进而获得更优的复杂度界。

Method: 作者引入参考活性迹B_ref，定义为从π_λ出发沿MYULA热子步平均的a_λ（g_λ的Hessian迹）。通过将主导离散化误差用B_ref而非全局界d/λ来控制，结合Moreau包络的弱Hessian分析，推导MYULA的W_2收敛复杂度。对结构化惩罚（分段线性、lasso型、分组、全变差），利用曲率-管估计建立与λ无关的B_ref界。并给出Moreau偏差界√m W_2(π_λ,π)≤G²λ/4。

Result: 得到MYULA在√m W_2距离下达到ε_alg精度所需迭代次数N的显式上界，其中复杂度依赖由L_f、τ_f、B_ref和M_λ刻画。通用估计B_ref≤d/λ给出Õ(ε^-3)精度依赖；对结构化惩罚，曲率-管估计使B_ref不依赖于λ，将精度依赖改进为Õ(ε^-2)。结合Moreau偏差界，选取λ≍ε/G²获得对目标π的端到端保证。

Conclusion: 通过引入参考活性迹替代全局曲率界，显著改进了MYULA在结构化稀疏/TV惩罚下的复杂度分析，从Õ(ε^-3)提升到Õ(ε^-2)，且不改变经典MYULA核本身。这揭示了该算法在非光滑问题中的实际性能远优于传统理论预测。

Abstract: We study the Moreau--Yosida unadjusted Langevin algorithm (MYULA) for the nonsmooth composite target \[ π(dx)\propto \exp\{-f(x)-g(x)\}\,dx, \qquad x\in\mathbb R^d, \] where \(f\) is \(m\)-strongly convex with \(L_f\)-Lipschitz gradient and \(g\) is convex and \(G\)-Lipschitz. Let \(g_λ\) be the Moreau envelope of \(g\), \(π_λ\) the corresponding smoothed target, and \(a_λ=\operatorname{tr}H_λ\), where \(H_λ\) is the a.e./weak Hessian of \(g_λ\). We show that the leading MYULA discretization error is controlled by the reference active trace \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\), the average of \(a_λ\) along the heat substep of one MYULA update started from \(π_λ\), rather than by the global curvature bound \(d/λ\). If \(M_λ\) is an a.e. upper bound for \(a_λ\), then, up to logarithmic factors, \[ N \lesssim \frac{1}{m} \left[ L_f + \frac{ τ_f+G^2+B_{\mathrm{ref}} }{ \varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}^2 } + \frac{M_λ}{\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}} \right], \qquad τ_f:= \sup_x\operatorname{tr}\nabla^2 f(x), \] iterations suffice to ensure \(\sqrt m\,W_2(μ_N,π_λ)\leq\varepsilon_{\mathrm{alg}}\), where \(μ_N\) is the law of the \(N\)-th iterate and \(W_2\) is the quadratic Wasserstein distance. We also prove the Moreau-bias bound \[ \sqrt m\,W_2(π_λ,π) \leq \frac{G^2λ}{4}. \] Thus, choosing \(λ\asymp\varepsilon/G^2\) gives an end-to-end guarantee for \(π\). The universal estimate \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\leq d/λ\) yields \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-3})\) accuracy dependence. For the structured piecewise-linear, lasso-type, group, and total-variation penalties considered here, curvature--tube estimates make \(B_{\mathrm{ref}}\) independent of \(λ\), yielding \(\widetilde O(\varepsilon^{-2})\) for the same classical MYULA kernel.

</details>


### [191] [Synthetic Persona Pretraining: Alignment from Token Zero](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13482)
*Julian Minder,Viktor Moskvoretskii,Raghav Singhal,Difan Jiao,Andy Arditi,Shaobo Cui,Yiderigun Borjigin,Kartik Bali,Stefan Krsteski,Harsh Raj,Huu Nguyen,Jannik Brinkmann,Ashton Anderson,Roland Aydin,Robert West*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出合成人格预训练（SPP）方法，在预训练的第一步（token zero）即安装期望的助手人格，通过在预训练文档上标注符合价值宪法的第一人称反思并用交叉熵损失训练，再通过人格绑定将这一人格与助手身份关联。在最高3B参数、500B token的预训练实验中，SPP显著提升了宪法遵循和越狱鲁棒性，降低了分布外道德困境中的错位率，同时保持能力不下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于语言模型的AI在自主场景中部署日益增多，其目标与人类价值观的对齐至关重要。然而，现有对齐（以及助手身份本身）通常在预训练之后才引入，此时行为先验已经建立，导致价值只是"薄薄覆盖层"而非根深蒂固，容易引发后续的错位。本文旨在探索一种不同范式：在预训练阶段就从第一个token起安装期望的助手人格，使价值观从根本上被内化。

Method: SPP方法包含三个步骤：（1）用规范性的价值宪法为标准预训练文档标注价值对齐的第一人称反思；（2）使用标准交叉熵损失对标准预训练文档及其反思同时进行预训练，从而在众多人格中植入期望人格；（3）在用户-助手对话数据上进行后训练，将期望人格与助手身份绑定（称为"人格绑定"）。此外，研究还设置了对照实验：仅在预训练末尾引入SPP，以验证早期干预的重要性。

Result: 在最高3B参数、500B token的预训练规模下，SPP方法提升了宪法遵循能力和越狱鲁棒性，降低了分布外道德困境中的错位率，同时保持了模型能力。对比实验表明，仅在预训练末尾引入SPP相比从token zero引入效果更弱：宪法遵循度更低、未能改变价值优先级、在困境中做出更少对齐的选择。早期干预的优势依赖于人格绑定，并且随着预训练预算的增加而增强。

Conclusion: 研究结果表明，早期塑造价值观对对齐至关重要，预训练阶段的人格干预是一种有效的对齐手段。SPP提供了一条从源头（预训练第一步）建立深厚价值基础的路径，而非事后浅层覆盖，为AI对齐提供了新的范式方向。

Abstract: As language-model-based AI is increasingly deployed in autonomous settings, aligning its goals and values with those of humans becomes critical. Today, alignment, and the assistant identity itself, are typically introduced only after pretraining, once behavioral priors are already established. This can make values a thin overlay, rather than deeply rooted, and facilitate subsequent misalignment. Pursuing a different paradigm, we introduce Synthetic Persona Pretraining (SPP), which installs the desired assistant persona from token zero in pretraining. First, we annotate pretraining documents with value-aligned first-person reflections derived from a normative value constitution. Second, we pretrain via the standard cross-entropy loss on standard pretraining documents as well as their reflections, which installs the desired persona among a multitude of other personas. Finally, we post-train on user-assistant dialogue data, which binds this desired persona to the assistant identity, a process we call persona binding. By pretraining models up to 3B parameters on 500B tokens, we show that SPP improves constitution following and jailbreak robustness, and reduces the misalignment rate in out-of-distribution moral dilemmas, while preserving capabilities. Early intervention matters: compared with alignment from token zero, introducing SPP only at the end of pretraining yields weaker constitution adherence, does not shift value priorities, and leads to less aligned choices in dilemmas. This advantage depends on persona binding and, importantly, increases with pretraining budget. Overall, our results show that shaping values early is critical for alignment and establish pretraining-time persona interventions as an effective approach to do so.

</details>


### [192] [Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique: A Spectral Framework for Equation Discovery, Approximation, and Integration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13504)
*Sabin Roman,Ljupco Todorovski,Saso Dzeroski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出稀疏正交回归技术（SORT），一种从含噪且非均匀采样数据中学习正交基展开的稀疏谱框架。SORT通过L1正则化回归直接从观测中估计展开系数，避免显式求积或解析内积计算，核心应用是数据驱动的常微分方程发现。实验表明SORT在基函数适配良好时匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线，且在稀疏采样、含噪导数估计和表示不匹配下更稳定。此外，SORT还支持非线性逼近和高维积分估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的符号回归、基于语法的发现以及SINDy式稀疏识别方法依赖于预定义的函数库，当库缺失问题特定的非线性项时模型可能失效。此外，现有方法通常需要进行显式数值积分或解析内积计算，这在含噪且非均匀采样的实际数据中较为脆弱。作者希望将问题从"在通用项中进行脆弱选择"转向"面向问题域进行基设计"，提供一种更稳定的中间表示。

Method: SORT采用稀疏谱框架：将向量场表示为所选正交基下的展开，通过L1正则化回归直接从观测数据估计展开系数，无需显式求积或解析内积计算。该方法避免了基于库的方法中需要枚举候选项的问题，而是将基设计作为显式的建模步骤。

Result: 在多个动力系统实验中，当基适配良好时SORT匹配或优于基于库的稀疏回归基线；在稀疏采样、含噪导数估计和基不匹配情况下表现出更稳定的退化。实验还表明主导低阶系数随模型阶数增加保持稳定，支持一致的模型增长。SORT还展示了非线性逼近和高维积分估计的能力。

Conclusion: SORT为系统识别、逼近和积分提供了可复用的中间表示，将基设计作为科学建模问题中的显式组成部分。它不是对符号回归方法的替代，而是互补路径：先恢复紧凑的谱表示，再据此引导对更简单解析形式的搜索。

Abstract: We develop the Sparse Orthogonal Regression Technique (SORT), a sparse spectral framework for learning orthonormal-basis expansions from noisy and irregularly sampled data. SORT estimates expansion coefficients directly from observations using L1-regularized regression, avoiding explicit quadrature or analytic inner-product evaluation. The central application is data-driven discovery of ordinary differential equations: vector fields are represented in chosen orthogonal bases and learned as sparse coefficient expansions. This provides a complementary route to symbolic regression, grammar-based discovery, and SINDy-style sparse identification by first recovering a compact spectral representation, which can later guide searches for simpler analytic forms. Across the dynamical-system experiments, SORT matches or improves upon library-based sparse-regression baselines when the basis is well adapted to the problem, and shows more stable degradation under sparse sampling, noisy derivative estimates, and representation mismatch. Specific examples illustrate why this representation is useful: if a finite library misses the problem-specific nonlinearity, the resulting model can fail. SORT is not immune to mismatch, but it shifts the problem away from brittle selection among generic terms to basis design adapted to the problem domain. The experiments also show that dominant low-order coefficients persist as model order increases, supporting order-consistent model growth. Beyond equation discovery, the same learned expansion supports nonlinear approximation and estimation of complex, high-dimensional integrals by coefficient readout. Overall, SORT provides a reusable intermediate representation for system identification, approximation, and integration, while making basis design an explicit part of the scientific modeling problem.

</details>


### [193] [Intern-S2-Preview: Scientific Agentic Foundation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13505)
*Lei Bai,Jiaqi Cao,Chiyu Chen,Guanzhou Chen,Kai Chen,Guangran Cheng,Erfei Cui,Xuanlang Dai,Shengyuan Ding,Shangheng Du,Yanhui Duan,Yue Fan,Youqing Fang,Quan Gan,Yuanyuan Gao,Jiaye Ge,Lixin Gu,Yuzhe Gu,Qipeng Guo,Junjun He,Xin Hong,Ming Hu,Zhouqi Hua,Haian Huang,Junhao Huang,Zixian Huang,Minxi Jin,Lingkai Kong,Alexander Lam,Zehao Li,Zonglin Li,Tianhao Liang,Dahua Lin,Junyao Lin,Tianyang Lin,Zhouhan Lin,Jiangning Liu,Jin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenran Liu,Yifei Liu,Yuhong Liu,Yuhong Liu,Zhoumianze Liu,Ziyan Liu,Ziyu Liu,Haijun Lv,Han Lv,Chengqi Lyu,Le Ma,Ningsheng Ma,Zerun Ma,Haoyang Peng,Runyu Peng,Jifei Shan,Zixin Shang,Kou Shi,Xiang Shi,Qisheng Su,Xuerui Su,Hao Sun,Xiao Sun,Yanan Sun,Yu Sun,Huanze Tang,Yinghao Tang,Wenhui Tian,Zhongbo Tian,Bingli Wang,Haomin Wang,Jiarui Wang,Jingzhi Wang,Rui Wang,Xiquan Wang,Yi Wang,Zhecan Wang,Ziyi Wang,Zun Wang,Rubin Wei,Lianyi Wu,Wen Wu,Yue Wu,Yuhan Wu,Zhenyu Wu,Zijian Wu,Shuhao Xing,Jun Xu,Xingle Xu,Xuenan Xu,Xiangchao Yan,Ziang Yan,Bowen Yang,Danni Yang,Lin Yang,Zhiqi Yang,Qian Yao,Haochen Ye,Peng Ye,Jinhui Yin,Jiashuo Yu,Dingbo Yuan,Fei Yuan,Yuhang Zang,Bo Zhang,Chao Zhang,Chen Zhang,Hongjie Zhang,Junming Zhang,Wenlong Zhang,Wenwei Zhang,Yiming Zhang,Zhuo Zhang,Ziyang Zhang,Haiteng Zhao,Penghao Zhao,Yibo Zhao,Zhonghan Zhao,Zhihang Zhong,Bowen Zhou,Peiheng Zhou,Xin Zhou,Xinyu Zhou,Yunhua Zhou,Dongsheng Zhu,Yicheng Zou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Intern-S2-Preview是一系列面向科学智能体的基础模型，通过多模态预训练、统一后训练管线（SFT、多任务RL、黑盒/白盒智能体RL及on-policy蒸馏）以及架构创新（397B时序建模扩展和Memory Decoder记忆增强路径），在科学、多模态、智能体及通用基准上取得领先或竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学发现越来越需要AI系统能够对异构模态的科学证据进行推理、与科学工具和环境交互，并能在长时间任务跨度中持续进步。现有模型在多模态科学理解、推理与长时程任务方面存在明显不足。

Method: 训练管线分两阶段：(1) 科学多模态预训练，基于渲染的科学文档、交错的图文数据和多样科学语料；(2) 统一后训练，包括SFT、可扩展多任务强化学习、黑盒与白盒智能体RL、on-policy蒸馏。在工程层面引入部分回放+离策略校正、自适应长度正则化、在线投机解码、鲁棒多任务优化及trace感知经验组装等技巧。架构上，Intern-S2-Preview-397B扩展了时间序列建模以支持从长序列理解到数值预测；Memory Decoder作为独立的记忆增强路径，可在冻结397B主干的情况下实现快速科学领域特化。

Result: 在科学、多模态、智能体和通用基准上，Intern-S2-Preview-397B取得了多个设置下的竞争性或领先结果。时间序列模块在SciTS基准上提升了科学信号理解与预测能力；独立的Intern-MemDec-4B扩展将Biology-Instructions平均分从56.92提升到60.32，而无需修改被冻结的397B主干。

Conclusion: Intern-S2-Preview展示了通过大规模多模态预训练与统一后训练管线构建科学智能体基础模型的可行路径，并验证了模块化扩展（时间序列建模和记忆解码器）在提升特定科学能力方面的有效性。

Abstract: Scientific discovery increasingly requires AI systems that can reason over scientific evidence of heterogeneous modalities, interact with scientific tools and environments, and sustain progress across long task horizons. We present Intern-S2-Preview, a series of scientific agentic foundation models designed to support multimodal scientific understanding, reasoning, generation, and long-horizon tasks. The training pipeline begins with scientific multimodal pre-training over rendered scientific documents, interleaved image-text data, and diverse scientific corpora. Starting from the pretrained checkpoint, we apply a unified post-training pipeline consisting of supervised fine-tuning, scalable multi-task reinforcement learning (RL), black- and white-box agentic RL, and on-policy distillation. This pipeline is supported by practical techniques that improve rollout and training stability and efficiency, including partial rollout with off-policy correction, adaptive length regularization, online speculative decoding, robust multi-task optimization, and trace-aware experience assembly for agentic tasks. At the architecture level, Intern-S2-Preview-397B extends time series modelling from efficient long-sequence understanding to numerical forecasting, while Memory Decoder is studied as a separate memory-augmented path for rapid scientific specialization without modifying the frozen 397B backbone. Evaluations across scientific, multimodal, agentic, and general-purpose benchmarks show that Intern-S2-Preview-397B achieves competitive or leading results in multiple settings. The time series modules improve scientific signal understanding and forecasting on SciTS, while the separate Intern-MemDec-4B extension improves the Biology-Instructions average score from 56.92 to 60.32 without modifying the frozen 397B backbone.

</details>


### [194] [Intervention-Aware Clinical World Model for Post-Op Outcome Forecasting in Cardiology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13518)
*Yunsung Chung,Yingshuo Liu,Abboud F. Hassan,Han Feng,Mary M. Maleckar,Nassir Marrouche,Jihun Hamm*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种干预感知的临床世界模型，通过结构化隐状态对患者术后不规则轨迹进行时间演进来预测房颤消融术后复发风险，在DECAAF-II数据集上实现AUROC 0.756、AUPRC 0.777的复发预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统临床预测模型将术后结局视为从基线测量到终点的一步映射，忽略了术后恢复过程中不规则轨迹（如临床观察、用药变化、重复干预、生理测量等异步记录）对风险动态评估的影响。

Method: 模型首先将基线影像编码为3D空间隐状态，然后利用手术背景信息、静态协变量、时间间隔和围事件生理嵌入来更新该状态。随访影像通过潜在预测目标提供仅训练期监督，不要求推理阶段有随访MRI强度数据。

Result: 在DECAAF-II数据集上的重复内部交叉验证中，模型复发预测AUROC为0.756，AUPRC为0.777；疤痕范围MAE为2.971个百分点，且推理时无需随访MRI强度数据。学习到的状态支持不同时间维度的复发风险查询以及对空白期记录的回顾性输入编辑。

Conclusion: 实验表明术后90天恢复窗口内的不规则记录能为长期复发风险提供临床上有意义的证据，所提出的干预感知世界模型能够有效利用这些异步事件进行动态风险评估，并支持灵活的风险查询与输入编辑。

Abstract: Many clinical prediction models treat post-intervention outcomes as a one-step mapping from baseline measurements to a future endpoint. However, recovery after a procedure often unfolds as an irregular trajectory: clinical observations, medication changes, repeat interventions, and physiological measurements are recorded asynchronously and can change risk assessment over time. We propose an intervention-aware clinical world model that represents each patient with a structured latent state and evolves it through time-ordered post-intervention events. The model first encodes baseline imaging into a 3D spatial latent state. It then updates this state using procedural context, static covariates, elapsed time, and peri-event physiological embeddings. Follow-up imaging provides training-only supervision through a latent forecasting objective. We apply the framework to atrial fibrillation ablation. During the 90-day recovery window, irregular post-procedure records provide clinically meaningful evidence for long-term recurrence risk. In repeated internal cross-validation on DECAAF-II, our model achieves AUROC 0.756 and AUPRC 0.777 for recurrence prediction. It also achieves a scar-extent MAE of 2.971 percentage points without requiring follow-up MRI intensities at inference. The learned state supports recurrence-risk queries at different horizons and retrospective input editing of blanking-period records.

</details>


### [195] [Vero: Can AI Agents Build Formally Verified Software Repositories?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13522)
*Zhe Ye,Hantao Lou,Yuechun Sun,Peiyang Song,Zhengxu Yan,Timothe Kasriel,Qingyang Zhang,Kaiyu Yang,Soonho Kong,Jingxuan He,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Vero是第一个在仓库级（repository-level）评估联合实现与证明合成的基准测试，包含来自真实世界多模块代码库的43个实例，涵盖Python、Dafny、Verus和Coq语言，并支持仅证明与代码+证明两种评估模式。最强智能体仅完整解决27/43个实例，表明当前Agent在仓库级验证软件合成方面仍有不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI编程智能体无法保证生成代码的正确性，而已有验证代码生成基准要么聚焦于单个函数，要么仅评估在给定实现基础上的证明生成。目前尚无基准评估智能体是否能跨真实多模块代码库做出连贯的实现与证明选择。

Method: Vero包含43个源自真实仓库的多模块Lean 4实例，每个实例预定义API接口、人工整理的正式规范和参考实现，支持仅证明与代码+证明两种评估模式。基准还包含审计机制，允许智能体正式证明规范不可满足或参考实现不正确，以发现并纠正整理过程中的潜在错误。

Result: 评估配备Lean工具链的前沿编码智能体配置，最强智能体仅完整解决43个实例中的27个，在最困难仓库中未关闭任何规范。

Conclusion: Vero为衡量仓库级验证软件合成进展提供了具体测试平台，当前智能体在此任务上仍表现不足，需要进一步研究以提升跨模块验证代码生成能力。

Abstract: AI agents are increasingly used for programming, but do not provide any guarantee on the correctness of generated code. Verified code generation, in which an agent produces both an implementation and a machine-checked proof of its specification, offers a stronger path toward trustworthy AI-generated software. Existing benchmarks in this direction either focus on individual functions or only evaluate proof generation with provided implementations. It is still an open question whether agents can make coherent implementation and proof choices across real multi-module codebases. To bridge this gap, we introduce Vero, the first benchmark to evaluate joint implementation and proof synthesis at the repository level. Vero contains 43 multi-module instances sourced from real-world repositories spanning Python, Dafny, Verus, and Coq, and covering diverse domains from cryptographic protocols to distributed systems. Each instance consists of a multi-module Lean 4 repository with predetermined API interfaces, manually curated formal specifications, and reference implementations, supporting both proof-only and code-and-proof evaluation modes. To improve benchmark reliability, Vero also includes an audit mechanism where agents are allowed to formally prove unsatisfiability of provided specification or incorrectness of reference code, which surfaces and corrects latent code and specification errors during curation. We evaluate frontier coding-agent configurations with Lean toolchain access. The strongest agent fully solves only 27 of 43 instances and closes no specifications on the hardest repositories. Vero provides a concrete testbed for measuring progress toward repository-scale verified software synthesis, where current agents still fall short. We release the benchmark, curation pipeline, and evaluation harness at https://github.com/sunblaze-ucb/vero.

</details>


### [196] [DARTree: Speculative Diffusion Decoding with Autoregressive Draft Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13524)
*Tianyi Li,Yaxin Luo,Xinyi Shang,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DARTree是一种无需训练的投机解码方法，将预训练的自回归校正头从链式扩展到树状结构，通过批量扩展和评分候选树、最佳优先剪枝选择验证树，在数学、代码和聊天基准上实现最高平均接受长度和加速比，最高可接受12.97个token/轮，实现9.73倍无损加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 投机解码通过并行验证多个草稿token来无损加速自回归语言模型。基于扩散的起草器虽能并行预测整个token块，但其逐位置分布是边际分布而非沿草稿路径条件化的；现有的循环校正仅在单一草稿链上引入因果信息，而扩散式树构建扩大了候选覆盖但未沿各分支进行校正。两者存在结合因果校正与树覆盖的空白。

Method: DARTree首先通过批量扩展和评分所有节点来构建固定宽度的候选树，然后仅应用最佳优先剪枝来选择验证树，从而将AR校正头的推理与顺序堆操作解耦。无需训练，将预训练的AR校正头从链式推广到树状结构。

Result: 在七个数学、代码和聊天基准上，DARTree在所有四种模型-温度配置中均取得最高的平均接受长度和加速比。每轮验证最多接受12.97个token，比DFlash多98.6%，比Domino多27.9%，最高实现9.73倍的无损加速（相对于本地测量的自回归解码）。

Conclusion: DARTree通过将AR校正从链式扩展到树式，同时利用树的候选覆盖广度和AR校正的因果信息，有效提升了投机解码的接受长度和加速性能，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: Speculative decoding losslessly accelerates autoregressive language models by verifying multiple draft tokens in parallel. Diffusion-based drafters further reduce proposal latency by predicting an entire token block in parallel, but their position-wise distributions are marginal rather than conditioned on tokens selected along each draft path. Existing recurrent correction incorporates causal information along a single draft chain, whereas diffusion-based tree construction broadens candidate coverage without carrying this correction along individual branches. We introduce DARTree, a training-free speculative decoding method that extends a pretrained AR correction head from chains to trees. DARTree first constructs a fixed-width candidate tree by expanding and scoring all nodes at each depth in a single batch, and then only applies best-first pruning to select the verification tree, decoupling AR-head inference from sequential heap operations. Across seven math, code, and chat benchmarks, DARTree achieves the highest average acceptance length and speedup in all four model--temperature configurations, accepting up to 12.97 tokens per verification round, 98.6\% more than DFlash and 27.9\% more than Domino in the same setting, and reaching up to 9.73$\times$ lossless speedup over locally measured autoregressive decoding.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Black-Box Knowledge Transfer across Distinct Feature Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12403)
*Oh-Ran Kwon,Daeyoung Ham*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种从预训练黑盒预测函数向异构输入空间迁移预测知识的方法，将目标回归函数分解为可迁移与不可迁移两部分，通过两步神经网络流程分别估计，在理论上给出优于非迁移方法的预测风险界，并扩展至多个黑盒聚合场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练黑盒预测函数蕴含着从大规模数据和大量计算中提炼的知识，但当可用的输入特征与黑盒所期望的特征不一致时（即异构输入空间），无法直接使用。现有方法缺乏系统性地将黑盒知识迁移到新特征空间的方案。

Method: 将目标回归函数分解为可迁移分量（可由黑盒提供信息）和不可迁移分量（捕获新空间的独特信息）。提出两步神经网络流程：第一步利用大量无标签特征对（桥接两个输入空间）估计可迁移分量，第二步利用有限标签估计不可迁移分量。框架可扩展到多个黑盒（各自拥有自己的输入空间），通过聚合降低预测误差。

Result: 推导出预测风险界，当不可迁移分量较小或光滑时，优于非迁移方法的预测风险；该过程能自适应两种情形。在额外条件下，估计器的最坏情况风险以严格更小的多项式阶优于仅依靠标注数据的极小极大风险。多黑盒聚合可降低预测误差。模拟数据和真实数据均验证了方法的实用价值。

Conclusion: 该方法有效实现了异构输入空间间的黑盒知识迁移，理论上具有更优的风险界，且通过多黑盒聚合进一步增强表现，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Pre-trained black-box predictive functions encode knowledge distilled from massive datasets and extensive computation. However, when the available input features differ from those the black box expects, direct use is infeasible. We introduce a method for transferring predictive knowledge from the black box to a new, heterogeneous input space. Our approach decomposes the target regression function into a transferable component, which the black box can inform, and a non-transferable component, which captures information unique to the new space. We propose a two-step neural network procedure, estimating the transferable component from abundant unlabeled feature pairs that bridge the two input spaces and the non-transferable component from limited labels. We derive prediction risk bounds that improve on those of a non-transfer alternative when the non-transferable component is small or smooth, and the procedure adapts to either case. Under additional conditions, the worst-case risk of our estimator is of strictly smaller polynomial order than the minimax risk of estimation from the labeled data alone. We extend the framework to multiple black boxes, each on its own input space, and show that aggregation can reduce prediction error relative to the best single black box. Simulated and real data demonstrate the practical value of the method.

</details>


### [198] [SSPO: Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization for Neural Combinatorial Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12443)
*Yuanyu Li,Jintao Xu,Zijiang Liu,Yongzhi Qi,Ningxuan Kang,Jianshen Zhang,Wei Qi,Chen Xie,Zuo-Jun Max Shen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出SSPO（结构感知相似性加权偏好优化）方法，通过相异度加权的留一法基线联合评分所有采样解，解决梯度信号极化与基线冗余两个问题，在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上取得一致改进，并已在京东实际生产系统部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经组合优化方法在利用并行采样解组信息时存在两类缺陷：偏好优化方法锚定单一最优解，丢弃其他解细粒度的质量与结构信号（梯度信号极化）；均值基线均匀加权所有解，使结构近似的解洪泛基线并保持高梯度方差（基线冗余）。

Method: SSPO对所有B个采样解通过相异度加权的留一法基线进行联合评分：结构上不同的解获得更高权重，以单一机制同时解决极化与冗余两大问题。基线采用零参数、问题自适应的解嵌入，由编码器已有的节点表示构建。

Result: 在TSP、EFL和JSP基准上相比最优锚定与均匀权重基线实现一致改进；与均匀RLOO的直接对比证实结构感知加权是主要改进驱动力；SSPO训练的EFL策略已在京东供应链生产系统中部署并验证规模化可行性。

Conclusion: 结构感知的相似性加权是一种有效且实用的采样解利用机制，能够同时优化梯度信号质量与降低方差，且在真实工业场景中具有部署价值。

Abstract: Neural combinatorial optimization (NCO) relies on parallel solution sampling for training, yet existing methods fail to fully exploit the rich information latent in a co-sampled solution group. Preference-optimization methods anchor on the single best solution and discard fine-grained quality and structural signal from all other peers-a failure we term gradient signal polarization. Mean-based baselines instead weight peers uniformly, so structurally near-identical peers flood the baseline with redundant information and keep gradient variance high-a failure we term baseline redundancy. We propose SSPO (Structure-Aware Similarity-Weighted Preference Optimization), which scores all $B$ sampled solutions jointly through a dissimilarity-weighted leave-one-out baseline: structurally distinct peers receive higher weight, resolving both failures in a single mechanism. The baseline uses zero-parameter, problem-adaptive solution embeddings built from the encoder's existing node representations. Experiments on TSP, EFL, and JSP benchmarks show consistent gains over prior best-anchor and uniform-weight baselines. A direct comparison against uniform RLOO on TSP and EFL confirms that structure-aware weighting is the primary driver of improvement. The SSPO-trained EFL policy has been deployed in a production facility-location system at JD$\mathord{.}$com, confirming practical viability at scale.

</details>


### [199] [Online Inference for Quantile Temporal Difference Learning in Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12973)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布强化学习中分位数时序差分学习（QTD）的统计推断问题，建立了同步和异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，并提出了基于随机缩放的在线推断方法，该方法的统计量沿整个QTD路径构造且无需存储完整轨迹，大幅降低了内存需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习中QTD算法的统计推断问题此前尚未得到充分研究，需要对QTD迭代的收敛性和推断方法提供理论基础，以满足实际应用中对高效、可靠推断的需求。

Method: 在假设可访问生成模型的条件下，分别建立同步与异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，证明QTD的平均迭代弱收敛于一个重标度的布朗运动；随后基于随机缩放方法构造渐近枢轴统计量，利用整条QTD路径的信息进行推断，且统计量可在线计算，无需存储全部迭代轨迹。

Result: 成功建立了同步与异步QTD的函数中心极限定理，提出了可在线计算、无需存储完整轨迹的随机缩放推断统计量，在保证推断有效性的同时显著降低了内存需求。

Conclusion: 该研究为分布强化学习中的QTD算法提供了系统的统计推断框架，在理论严谨性与计算效率之间取得了良好平衡，为实际应用中的高效推断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: In this paper, we study how to perform statistical inference for quantile temporal difference learning (QTD) in distributional reinforcement learning. Assuming access to a generative model, we first establish functional central limit theorems for both synchronous and asynchronous QTD, which show that the averaged iterates of QTD converge weakly to a rescaled Brownian motion. We next provide online inference methods. Based on random scaling, the inference procedure constructs an asymptotically pivotal statistic for inference by using the information along the whole QTD path. Meanwhile, the proposed statistic can be computed online without storing the entire trajectory of QTD iterates. This substantially reduces the memory requirement and enables efficient statistical inference in distributional reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [200] [Statistical Properties of Robust Learning under Distributional Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13133)
*Zhiyi Li,Xiaojie Mao,Yunbei Xu,Ruohan Zhan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了分布偏移下鲁棒学习框架（DRO和RS）在目标环境中的有限样本泛化误差界，提出了基于部分偏移信息的信息引导超参数校准方法，并应用于网络批量规划问题，填补了鲁棒学习方法在分布偏移下统计性质和系统比较方面的空白。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DRO和RS的有限样本保证通常只在源环境或对抗性最坏情况（模糊集上）下建立，缺乏在目标（偏移后）环境中的泛化误差分析以及两种方法的系统比较，这是分布偏移下鲁棒学习理论的一个关键空白。

Method: 首先推导DRO和RS在偏移目标环境下的有限样本泛化误差界，显式刻画鲁棒超参数在降低偏移敏感性与引入正则化惩罚之间的权衡，并避免Wasserstein经验集中度的维度灾难；其次，在可获得部分偏移信息（如偏移幅度或方向）时，提出信息引导的超参数校准方法，并在相同信息条件下比较两种方法；最后将该框架应用于网络批量规划问题。

Result: 在部分信息机制下，DRO和RS表现出互补的理论与经验行为；所推导的误差界明确刻画了鲁棒性与正则化惩罚之间的权衡关系；网络批量规划案例展示了鲁棒策略如何应对需求分布的正向偏移。

Conclusion: 本文填补了鲁棒学习方法在分布偏移下统计性质的理论空白，为DRO与RS的比较提供了原则性基础，并为实践中的超参数选择提供了信息引导的校准策略。

Abstract: Distributional shifts arise when the target deployment environment differs from the source environment that generated the training data. Robust learning frameworks such as Distributionally Robust Optimization (DRO) and Robust Satisficing (RS) aim to address this challenge, yet their finite-sample guarantees under such shifts, and their systematic comparison, remain underexplored: existing analyses typically establish guarantees either in the source environment or for adversarial worst-case performance over an ambiguity set. This paper instead studies generalization error in the target environment---the excess loss under the shifted target distribution. Our contributions are threefold. First, we derive finite-sample generalization error bounds in the shifted target environment for both DRO and RS. These bounds explicitly characterize the trade-off between reduced sensitivity to shift and the regularization penalty induced by each method's robustness hyperparameter, and they avoid the curse of dimensionality associated with Wasserstein empirical concentration. Second, when partial shift information such as shift magnitude or direction is available, we propose information-directed hyperparameter calibrations and compare the two methods given the same information. Under these calibrations, and in the partial-information regimes we study, DRO and RS exhibit complementary theoretical and empirical behavior. Finally, we apply the framework to a network lot-sizing problem, using it to interpret how robust policies respond to positive shifts in the demand distribution. Together, these results fill a gap in understanding the statistical properties of robust learning methods under distributional shifts and provide a principled basis for comparing DRO and RS.

</details>


### [201] [High-dimensional networks and mean squared error for possibly misspecified models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13171)
*Lourens Waldorp*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文探讨了高维网络分析中邻域选择（neighbourhood selection）的问题。研究表明，在参数远多于观测值的高维场景下，最小描述长度（MDL）方法能够保守（低假阳性率）地估计每个节点的邻域，而传统的Lasso、AIC、BIC等方法会产生大量虚假边（高假阳性率）。文章将岭参数对均方误差的影响与机器学习中的"双下降"（double descent）现象联系起来，证明在高维设置下，MDL在真实模型为线性或非线性时都能实现正确的邻域选择或更小的邻域（低假阳性率）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络分析中为避免遗漏重要变量及其连接，越来越多的变量被纳入分析，导致高维（参数远超观测数）场景的出现。在高维情况下，传统的邻域选择方法（如Lasso、AIC、BIC）会产生大量虚假边，假阳性率过高，因此需要一种能够保守估计邻域（低假阳性率）的方法。

Method: 作者分析了线性模型框架下的邻域选择，区分了真实模型为线性和非线性两种情况。在线性情况下邻域选择效果尚可；在非线性情况下则需要惩罚项处理高维问题。作者研究了岭参数（ridge parameter）对均方误差的影响，发现其导致低测试方差进而产生大量边的邻域。文章将这一现象与机器学习中的"双下降"现象相结合，证明在高维设置下，模型的体积（模型空间大小）需要纳入惩罚项。最终证明最小描述长度（MDL）方法能够实现正确或更小的邻域选择。

Result: 作者证明了最小描述长度（MDL）在高维设置下能够在两种情况下（真实模型正确假设为线性或错误假设为线性）实现正确的邻域选择或更小的邻域（低假阳性率），而Lasso、AIC、BIC等传统方法则产生高假阳性率的虚假边。文章还揭示了岭参数与均方误差之间的关系，并将其与机器学习的双下降现象联系起来。

Conclusion: 在高维网络分析中，传统的邻域选择方法（Lasso、AIC、BIC）会产生大量虚假连接，而最小描述长度（MDL）方法能够提供保守的（低假阳性率）邻域估计。关键在于惩罚项需要纳入模型空间的体积，这与机器学习中双下降现象的洞见一致。这为高维网络中的邻域选择提供了理论上更可靠的方法。

Abstract: To avoid missing important variables and their connections in networks, more and more variables are included in network analysis. Here we show that in a setting with many more parameters than observations (high-dimensional) it is possible to get a conservative (i.e., low false positive rate) estimate of the neighbourhood for each node (which connections are in the network). A neighbourhood is often estimated with a linear model, and this leads to two interesting cases: (i) If the true model is linear, then neighbourhood selection work reasonably well, and (ii) if the true model is nonlinear, then neighbourhood selection requires a penalty for the high dimensions. Here we show the impact of the ridge parameter on the mean squared error, and how this leads to low test variance and hence to neighbourhoods with large numbers of edges. We connect these insights with results from machine learning, where the so-called double descent (when more parameters are included than observations, the mean squared error goes down a second time) has put the traditional view on model selection upside down. Essentially, for adequate neighbourhood selection in models with a large number of parameters, the volume of the model space needs to be included in the penalty. Most neighbourhood selection methods (e.g., Lasso, AIC, BIC) lead to spurious edges (high false positive rate), but we prove that in the high-dimensional setting, minimum description length leads to correct neighbourhood selection or smaller (low false positive rates) in both cases when either the model is correctly or incorrectly assumed linear

</details>


### [202] [Sinkhorn Linearization and the Spectral Proxy: Unifying the Statistical and Algorithmic Theory of Feature-Parameterized Inverse Optimal Transport via a Single Spectral Sandwich](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13201)
*Han Dong,Jiaming Li,Yongqiang Gong,Ruixi Li,Yin Liu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文系统建立了特征参数化成本函数下的逆最优传输（IOT）统计与算法理论，核心贡献是Sinkhorn线性化技术及其谱代理公式，并据此推导出四个定理和一个观测，涵盖可辨识性、稀疏恢复、适定性与收敛性等关键性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆最优传输（IOT）旨在从观测的传输计划或数据中反推成本函数参数，但在特征参数化成本C_theta(i,j)=-theta^T phi(i,j)下，缺乏系统的统计与算法理论支撑。已有工作多关注无参数或有限维设置，缺少对可辨识性、估计一致性和优化收敛性的严格刻画，因此需要建立统一的核心技术框架。

Method: 提出Sinkhorn线性化，即熵正则OT计划对成本参数的隐函数敏感性分析，并构造其谱代理公式（谱精确且几何透明）。在切空间上的受限Hessian满足谱三明治不等式(pi_min/epsilon)I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon)I，由此推导核心界sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma))。基于此核心界建立四个定理：T1全局单射可辨识性（商掉规范核，维度上界F <= (K-1)^2）；T2 l1惩罚估计的稀疏一致性（在不可表示性与得分集中条件下，失败概率指数衰减）；T3特征矩映射的强单调性与逆映射Lipschitz连续性；T4局部强凸性保证梯度下降单调收敛；以及O5关于模型误设定的收敛与投影映射Hölder连续性评估。

Result: 建立了完整的IOT理论体系：可辨识性方面，参数theta在规范核的商空间上全局单射；稀疏性方面，l1惩罚估计在温和条件下以指数概率恢复真实支撑；适定性方面，特征矩映射强单调且逆映射具有显式Lipschitz常数；收敛性方面，给出了保证梯度下降单调收敛的局部强凸参数下界；误设定情形下，估计量收敛到OT模型投影，投影映射的Hölder连续性为设置依赖的经验指数alpha_eff in (0,1)。

Conclusion: 本文通过Sinkhorn线性化这一核心技术，为特征参数化成本的IOT问题提供了统一且严格的统计与算法理论。谱三明治不等式是贯穿全部结果的核心机制，使得可辨识性、稀疏一致性、适定性与优化收敛性可在同一框架下证明。这为IOT在机器学习与计量经济学中的实际应用奠定了理论基础。

Abstract: We develop the statistical and algorithmic theory of inverse optimal transport (IOT) under the feature-parameterized cost C_theta(i,j) = -theta^T phi(i,j). The core technical contribution is the Sinkhorn linearization -- the implicit-function sensitivity of the entropic OT plan to the cost -- together with its spectral proxy, a formula that is spectrally exact yet geometrically transparent.
  The restricted Hessian on the tangent space satisfies the spectral sandwich (pi_min/epsilon) I <= H_T^{-1} <= (pi_max/epsilon) I, yielding the single core bound sigma_min >= (pi_min/(a_max epsilon)) sqrt(lambda_min(Sigma)) that drives the entire theory. On this core we establish four theorems and one observation.
  T1 (identifiability): theta is globally injective on the quotient of the gauge kernel, with dimension bound F <= (K-1)^2. T2 (sparsistency): the l1-penalized estimator recovers the true support under irrepresentability and score concentration, with exponential failure probability. T3 (well-posedness): the feature-moment map M(theta) = Phi^T x_theta is strongly monotone, and the inverse is Lipschitz with constant L <= epsilon ||Phi^T S_a||_op / (pi_min lambda_min(Sigma)). T4 (convergence): local strong convexity with mu >= pi_min^2 lambda_min(Sigma) / epsilon^2 guarantees monotone gradient descent convergence. O5 (misspecification): the estimator converges to the OT-model projection of the truth; the Holder continuity of the projection map is assessed numerically, yielding setting-dependent empirical exponents alpha_eff in (0,1).

</details>


### [203] [Wasserstein Filtering: A Sample Selection Method for Robust Distribution Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13418)
*Yikai Xu,Zhao Chen,Jian Huang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出Wasserstein Filtering (WF)框架，通过丢弃部分可疑样本并基于剩余数据的经验测度来恢复干净的数据分布。WF通过最大化子集经验分布与完全污染经验分布之间的Wasserstein距离来隔离和移除几何上影响大的离群点，并证明了在FELP污染模型下达到极小极大最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际数据集中常含有被污染的样本，这些污染会显著影响下游任务（如生成建模、统计推断等）。现有方法缺乏一个统一的、模型无关的预处理框架来有效恢复底层干净分布。核心挑战在于如何设计一种既能在理论上保证最优性、又在计算上可处理的样本选择策略。

Method: 提出Wasserstein Filtering (WF)框架，核心思想是选择最大化子集经验分布与完全污染经验分布之间Wasserstein距离的样本子集，从而优先隔离并移除几何上影响显著的离群点。为计算可行性，设计了三种算法：边际筛选方案SinkMarg、基于熵最优传输的联合优化算法SinkWF、以及基于切片Wasserstein近似的SlicedWF。理论上引入Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP)污染模型，刻画由良好分离的离群点和局部不可区分扰动组成的污染。

Result: 在理论上，证明WF估计器在FELP污染模型下、对有界协方差分布族达到极小极大最优性。在实验中，WF在合成数据集、基准异常检测套件以及扩散模型的鲁棒生成学习上均表现优异，能提供有竞争力的离群点检测性能，并在重度污染下为生成建模带来实质性下游收益。

Conclusion: WF是一个高度实用、模型无关的预处理工具，兼具理论最优性保证和良好的实证表现。它在离群点检测和鲁棒生成建模方面展现出了广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: Given a dataset where a portion of the samples are contaminated, our goal is to recover the underlying clean population distribution. To this end, we propose Wasserstein Filtering (WF), a novel sample selection framework that discards a fraction of suspicious samples and estimates the target distribution using the empirical measure of the remaining data. The core insight is to select a subset of samples whose empirical distribution maximizes its Wasserstein distance to the fully contaminated empirical distribution, thereby preferentially isolating and removing geometrically influential outliers. To render this optimization computationally tractable, we introduce three algorithms: a marginal screening scheme, SinkMarg, and two joint optimization algorithms, SinkWF and SlicedWF, leveraging entropic optimal transport and sliced Wasserstein approximations, respectively. On the theoretical front, we introduce the Far Exclusion and Local Projection (FELP) contamination model, which characterizes corruptions consisting of well-separated outliers and locally indistinguishable perturbations. Under this model, we prove that the WF estimator achieves minimax optimality over distribution families with bounded covariance. Extensive numerical experiments on synthetic datasets, benchmark anomaly detection suites, and robust generative learning with diffusion models demonstrate that WF serves as a highly practical, model-agnostic preprocessing tool. It delivers competitive outlier detection performance and provides substantial downstream benefits for generative modeling under heavy contamination.

</details>


### [204] [Bagging Robustly Learns VC Classes with Linear Sample Complexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13514)
*Omar Montasser*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了对抗鲁棒学习问题，证明VC类可以用与VC维度d线性关系的样本复杂度进行对抗鲁棒学习，相比之前指数级上界有巨大改进。该成果通过将bagging（自举聚合）与鲁棒经验风险最小化（RERM）相结合的简单非适当算法实现，并给出下界证明该方法的Oracle调用复杂度是最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前Montasser等人（2019）对对抗鲁棒可学习性给出的上界是指数级的（相对于VC维度），这在实际中不可行。作者希望探索是否能以更高效的样本复杂度实现对抗鲁棒学习，特别是在Oracle模型下是否可以用线性于VC维度的样本量完成学习任务。

Method: 作者提出一个非适当（improper）学习算法，其灵感来源于Breiman（1996）的经典bagging（自举聚合）启发式方法。算法从训练数据中采样O(d*)个独立bootstrap样本，在每个样本上运行RERM（鲁棒经验风险最小化），最后对这些RERM预测结果取多数投票。其中d*是VC类的对偶VC维度（dual VC dimension）。

Result: 1）证明了VC类在对抗鲁棒学习下具有线性于VC维度d的样本复杂度，比先前指数上界实现了指数级改进；2）该上界通过对偶VC维度d*表征；3）补充了下界结果：在Oracle模型中任何学习器至少需要Ω(d*)次RERM Oracle调用，即使训练样本无限多，表明该算法的Oracle查询复杂度是最优的、不可避免的。

Conclusion: 这项工作正面解决了对抗鲁棒可学习性中关于样本复杂度的开放问题，证明了在Oracle模型下对抗鲁棒学习并不需要指数级样本，同时揭示了bagging这一经典启发式在对抗鲁棒学习中的理论价值，为设计高效对抗鲁棒学习算法提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We revisit the problem of learning predictors robust to adversarial examples at test-time. We prove that VC classes are adversarially robustly learnable with sample complexity linear in the VC dimension $d$, providing an exponential improvement over the previous upper bound of Montasser, Hanneke, and Srebro (2019). Remarkably, this result is achieved with a simple improper algorithm that combines the classic heuristic bagging (bootstrap aggregation) of Breiman (1996) with robust empirical risk minimization (RERM). Our algorithm computes RERMs on $O(d^\star)$ independent bootstrap samples and outputs their majority vote, where $d^\star$ denotes the dual VC dimension. We complement this result with a lower bound showing that this is unavoidable: in general, any learner in this oracle model requires $Ω(d^\star)$ calls to an RERM oracle, even when given arbitrarily many training examples.

</details>
